# Equipment related | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related.md)
.

1.Get device list[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-1.get-device-list)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/get

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

Get the specified device. Multiple devices are separated by commas. Without this parameter, all devices can be obtained.

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Get a list of all devices

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/get'

Get the specified device

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/get?id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71,5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:52'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Get a list of all devices

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/get

Get the specified device

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/get \
      --form id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71,5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:52

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Get a list of all devices

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/get",
        "data": {
        }
    }'

Get the specified device

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/get",
        "data": {
            "id": "9A:97:4E:C9:54:71,5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:52"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo)

Copy

      {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          {\
            "air_handle": 121092144,\
            "air_pid": 10952,\
            "rotate": 0,\
            "state": 0,\
            "imgw": 750,\
            "imgh": 1334,\
            "gid": 0,\
            "air_ratio": 0,\
            "air_fps": 0,\
            "air_refresh": 0,\
            "air_img_fps": 0,\
            "air_audio": 0,\
            "name": "",\
            "srv_name": "`iMouseXP-7827",\
            "width": "375",\
            "height": "667",\
            "ip": "192.168.9.118",\
            "mac": "00:E0:99:D9:92:82",\
            "user_name": "iPhone",\
            "version": "15.7.3",\
            "model": "iPhone8,1",\
            "deviceid": "48:3B:38:CD:02:F0",\
            "device_name": "iPhone 6s",\
            "location": "",\
            "location_crc": "B0DBD800",\
            "vid": "720D",\
            "pid": "6E22",\
            "uid": "6E2224BD",\
            "gname": "Ungrouped",\
            "uver": "4.4"\
          }\
        ],
        "code": 0,

      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "success",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "\/device\/get"

*   **Response data data field description** list array records the acquired devices.

\-

\-

uver

2.Get group list[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-2.get-group-list)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming-1)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/group/get

Request function name

id

String

Group ID

Get the specified group. Multiple groups are separated by commas. Without this parameter, all groups can be obtained.

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Get a list of all groupings

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/group/get'

Get the specified group

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/group/get?id=1012519501'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Get a list of all groupings

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/group/get

Get the specified group

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/group/get \
      --form id=1012519501

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Get a list of all groupings

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/group/get",
        "data": {
           
        }
    }'

Get the specified group

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/group/get",
        "data": {
             "id": "1012519501"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-1)

Copy

      {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          {\
            "id": "223347827",\
            "name": "test0"\
          },\
          {\
            "id": "223347853",\
            "name": "test1"\
          },\
          {\
            "id": "223347870",\
            "name": "test2"\
          }\
        ],
        "code": 0,
        "id": "",
        "message": "success"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "success",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "/device/group/get"
    }

*   **Response data data field description** list array records the obtained groups.

4.Setting up the device[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-4.setting-up-the-device)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#calling)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/set

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

You need to set the device id, separate multiple devices with commas

name

String

Custom Name

\-

vid

String

im hardware vid

The pid and hardware id must be set at the same time. Setting the hardware id can only set a single device.

pid

String

im hardware pid

and vid must be set at the same time

location

String

Mouse parameters

\-

location\_crc

String

Mouse parameters crc

\-

gid

String

Group id

\-

There are two parameters: mouse parameter and mouse parameter crc. Only one of them is needed. It is recommended to use location\_crc

Mouse parameters iMouse hardware adapts to different models of mobile phones through different mouse parameters, and users can collect them themselves

The mouse parameter crc has collected the crc value of the corresponding parameter in the mouse parameter library

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Set a custom device name

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/set?id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71&name=test002'

Setting up custom device names and groups simultaneously

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/set?id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71&name=test001&gid=0'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Set a custom device name

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/set \
        --form id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71 \
        --form name=test002

Setting up custom device names and groups simultaneously

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/set \
      --form id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71 \
        --form name=test001 \
        --form gid=0

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/set",
        "data": {
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E",
        "name": "test002"
        }
    }'

Setting up custom device names and groups simultaneously

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/set",
        "data": {
            "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E",
            "name": "test002",
            "gid": "0"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-3)

Copy

      {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E"\
        ],
        "code": 0,
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E",
        "message": "成功"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "成功",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "/device/set"
    }

*   **Response data Data field description** list array Returns the unique ID of all successfully set devices
    

5.Deleting a device[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-5.deleting-a-device)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming-3)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/del

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

The device ID needs to be deleted. Multiple devices are separated by commas.

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/del?id=90:81:58:EE:D1:9E,DC:2B:2A:14:2A:F6'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/del \
        --form id=90:81:58:EE:D1:9E,DC:2B:2A:14:2A:F6

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/del",
        "data": {
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E,DC:2B:2A:14:2A:F6"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-4)

Copy

      {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E"\
        ],
        "code": 0,
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E,DC:2B:2A:14:2A:F6",
        "message": "成功"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "成功",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "/device/del"
    }

*   **Response data Data field description** list array Returns the unique ID of all successfully deleted devices
    

6.Set up grouping[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-6.set-up-grouping)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming-4)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/group/set

Request function name

id

String

Group id

Filling 0 in id means adding a new group

name

String

Group Name

\-

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Add a new group

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/group/set?id=0&name=g1'

Set the specified id group name

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/group/set?id=1000630707&name=g3'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Add a new group

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/group/set \
        --form id=0 \
        --form name=g2

Set the specified id group name

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/group/set \
      --form id=222440499 \
        --form name=test001 

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Add a new group

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/group/set",
        "data": {
        "id": "0",
        "name": "xxx"
        }
    }'

Set the specified id group name

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/group/set",
        "data": {
            "id": "222440499",
            "name": "test002"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-5)

Copy

     {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          {\

            "name": "1111"\
          }\
        ],

        "id": "0",
        "message": "成功"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "成功",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "\/device\/group\/set"
    }

Group ID

Multiple groups are separated by commas.

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/group/del?id=1145527833,1145533497'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/group/del \
        --form id=34343488,2323233

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/group/del",
        "data": {
        "id": "34343488,2323233"
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-6)

Copy

    {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          "1145527833",\
          "1145533497"\
        ],
        "code": 0,
        "id": "1145527833,1145533497",
        "message": "成功"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "成功",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "/device/group/del"
    }

*   **Response data Data field description** list array returns the IDs of all groups that have been successfully deleted
    

8.Set device screen projection configuration[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#id-8.set-device-screen-projection-configuration)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming-6)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/airplay/set

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

You need to set the device id, separate multiple devices with commas

air\_ratio

Integer

Screen projection resolution

0,720,1080,1400,1920,2650

air\_refresh

Integer

Screen refresh rate

0-60

air\_fps

Integer

Screen projection frame rate

0-30

air\_audio

Integer

Screen projection sound

0 (Use Global), 1 (Off), 2 (On)

air\_img\_fps

Integer

Cast image conversion frame rate

0-30

If not set or set to 0, the screen projection will be disconnected immediately after the global parameter setting is used, and will take effect after the next screen projection.

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Set the screen mirroring frame rate

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/airplay/set?id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71&air_fps=0'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Set the screen mirroring frame rate

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/device/airplay/set \
        --form id=9A:97:4E:C9:54:71 \
        --form air_fps=15

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Set the screen mirroring frame rate

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/device/airplay/set",
        "data": {
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E",
        "air_fps":15
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/equipment-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo-7)

Copy

      {
      "data": {
        "code": 0,
        "id": "90:81:58:EE:D1:9E",
        "message": "成功"
      },
      "status": 200,
      "message": "成功",
      "msgid": 0,
      "fun": "/device/airplay/set"
    }

*   **Response data data field description** None

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/device/sort/set

The device list and console list you get will be sorted according to your settings

sort\_index

Integer

Sorting Index

This id corresponds to the console header list, starting from 1

sort\_value

Integer

Sort id values

0 unsorted, -1 up, 1 down

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/device/sort/set?sort_index=1&sort_value=-1'

      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \

      --form sort_index=1
      --form sort_value=1

# Panda Android Screen Projection | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart.md)
.

### 1.Product interface[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart#id-1.product-interface)

![xiaowei Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FEqMCwwZXQetkC0fIYYec%252Fmain2.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da559b4f9-e02d-4544-bfab-792fa883a47b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f4271f3c38da5f5feb3fbbd96ccac1c6&sv=3)

some3c.com

Panda Screen Mirroring is a software used to connect and manage Android phones on a computer. It is specially designed for batch screen mirroring management of Android phones. It can greatly improve the operating efficiency of managing Android phones on a computer, especially in batch operations, automated operations, In terms of remote operation, efficiency has been significantly improved.

### 2\. Application scenarios[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart#id-3dbf0c11)

Panda Screencast is a connection tool that uses a USB cable or other connection methods to project the Android phone screen onto the computer screen through video streaming. The computer mouse is used instead of fingers to slide and click on the phone screen. The purpose is to facilitate mobile application developers to view and operate mobile application software on the computer. The connection between Android phone and computer is the connection method that comes with the Android system. Panda Screencast only plays a connecting role and does not make any modifications or damage to the mobile phone system, nor does it provide any auxiliary functions for any mobile application. It only provides the most basic connection function between Android and Windows.

### 3\. Multi-language support[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart#id-8b25bff8)

![xiaowei Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F7DlwYALCruUTUml5Gi6q%252F%25E8%25AF%25AD%25E8%25A8%2580.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D28828c68-6b09-4c88-bfa0-6ab8735cb8c5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3d3704f21b67969d0095587ac113723e&sv=3)

some3c.com

As the software continues to be updated, more language support will be added in the future, whichever is included in the actual software.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

VIVO series phones need to turn on \[USB debugging\] \[USB simulated click\] settings to control

![phone farm setup some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fh32GX4aitfbFTi9RQJTx%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc312afae-478c-4de6-b384-ed58083b8cdb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a06ef7a19cb03a7808f6feb4c7f3a340&sv=3)

some3c.com

And turn on "USB Safety Permissions", then you enable USB debugging successfully

![phone farm setup some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FaO0YIbjuFjXLRWa1EAGu%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df7723f29-1d82-457c-87c0-6d37b49643ba&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fcb7e3ea280c98020071b73a9461ac28&sv=3)

3.Huawei Honor phone cannot be recognized?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-3.huawei-honor-phone-cannot-be-recognized)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Huawei or Honor phones need to download the corresponding mobile assistant on their official websites (ignore for other brands of phones)

Huawei Mobile Assistant: https://consumer.huawei.com/cn/support/hisuite/

Honor Mobile Assistant: https://www.honor.com/cn/tech/honor-suite/

1.  Install the mobile assistant on your computer
    
2.  Use a USB data cable to successfully connect the phone and assistant (the mobile assistant has a connection tutorial)
    
3.  The assistant will push the Huawei or Honor mobile phone driver to the phone, click to install
    
4.  After the driver is successfully installed, turn off the mobile assistant and re-plug the USB cable
    
5.  If it still doesn't work, try restarting the phone
    

4\. Computer Problems[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-4.-computer-problems)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1\. What is the upper limit of device connection? Panda Android Mirror Screen Casting Software itself has no limit on the number of connected mobile phones. The number of connected devices is determined by the user's computer motherboard or router.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-1.-what-is-the-upper-limit-of-device-connection-panda-android-mirror-screen-casting-software-itself)

![Phone farm software some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fq2o90M91wHtiAU7XgBZY%252Fmain1.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0cc9429e-a96e-426a-8e3f-11463a5b229e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=03861d7a36a00a41582e0ce5b1d9ba09&sv=3)

some3c.com

USB connection mode

1.  Theoretically, the motherboard of an ordinary home computer host supports a maximum of 127 USB connections, but most motherboard manufacturers limit the number of USB connections to 40. Therefore, under normal circumstances, a home computer host can only connect to 40 devices.
    
2.  The motherboard of a commercial server usually adopts the X79 or X99 architecture, which has no limit on the number of USB connections and can be unlimited in theory.
    

OTG or WIFI connection mode

1.  If you use network connection modes such as OTG or WIFI, the number of connections is not limited by the computer motherboard, so there is no need to consider the problem of the computer motherboard. But it is limited by the performance of the router.
    
2.  Usually, home routers can only support up to 20 devices for stable use, and more will cause frequent disconnection. Enterprise routers can usually support up to 40 devices for stable use, and more will cause frequent disconnection.
    
3.  The most cost-effective router solution is soft routing. In theory, a soft routing device can support hundreds of devices for stable use at the same time. Of course, the premise is that your bandwidth must be sufficient. If the upstream and downstream speeds of the bandwidth are not enough, it will still be stuck.
    

### 2\. What if the device cannot be detected?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-2.-what-if-the-device-cannot-be-detected)

First, check whether the computer (open this computer) recognizes the device. If the computer does not recognize the device, the software cannot recognize it, so you need to find the problem with the computer. If there is a device in the computer and the software cannot recognize it, there are probably the following reasons Cause analysis:

1.  The mobile phone USB debugging switch is not turned on;
    
2.  The device has not installed the mobile phone driver;
    
3.  Adb conflict, there are multiple different versions of Adb.exe files in the system. Solution:
    
4.  Check USB debugging in the mobile phone developer options.
    
5.  Refer to the mobile phone driver installation problem to install the driver.
    
6.  Refer to the Adb conflict resolution method.
    

### 3\. What to do if USB controller resources are insufficient?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-3.-what-to-do-if-usb-controller-resources-are-insufficient)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fzf8qn6IhSc0WmDDnA2Dn%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db38b36d7-1e37-467e-b9fa-80451811b747&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a47486a2a0698025bd965b2be814dd70&sv=3)

some3c.com

First turn off the \[XHCI\] item under \[USB\] in \[BIOS\], then try to change the USB port of the computer, and finally try to use a specific USB expansion card. If some motherboards have extra card slots, you can buy a USB expansion card. One expansion card can carry about 15 devices. It depends on the computer motherboard.

### 4.What should I do if the Panda device is detected and it says it is not authorized?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-4.what-should-i-do-if-the-panda-device-is-detected-and-it-says-it-is-not-authorized)

1.  Find a merchant to obtain the adb authorization file (adbkey, adbkey.pub),
    
2.  Put these two files in the C:\\Users\\Administrator.android folder, overwrite them, and then restart the computer.
    

![Phone farm software some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FpaW6rdXkXX5SCR2ByLJo%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df1ac0885-03aa-4ae6-9c43-ad17b40aca87&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9680226997a12547402c298fa93e2ef1&sv=3)

5.How to check device status using the device list?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-5.how-to-check-device-status-using-the-device-list)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click on the panel "Devices" to view the status of all devices.

![iphone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FERqFxxyNMjPA1QUlXwd3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da726a41d-61f6-4878-a14c-49e667cdd10b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0d859c1670d9372d1d42ed045030aab7&sv=3)

6.What should I do if it prompts that the device is disconnected from the server?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-6.what-should-i-do-if-it-prompts-that-the-device-is-disconnected-from-the-server)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![iphone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FTzfoQI2JPFC9GpnEUcMf%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9e01f599-f2f8-49ed-8975-84d064d0a105&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b05f9622049bffbb7ad997a4c1cad5b5&sv=3)

**Common reasons**

The user's local computer has incorrect time or time zone, which will cause disconnection with the server

**Uncommon reasons**

The user has opened a VPN, which causes the connection delay with Panda to be too high.

The user has opened a proxy server in the computer settings.

The user has opened a packet capture tool, which causes Panda to fail to connect.

**Solution**

1.Change the computer local time to the current time 2.Exit VPN, IP proxy tool, and then restart Panda, 3.Exit the packet capture tool (such as Wireshark, Fiddler, etc.) 4.Reconnect your computer to the Internet.

7.What should I do if the screen goes black or the connection is disconnected when using a certain type of app?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-7.what-should-i-do-if-the-screen-goes-black-or-the-connection-is-disconnected-when-using-a-certain-t)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Problem

1.  The phone screen goes black when paying
    
2.  The phone screen goes black when entering the password
    

Reason

1.  The phone system's security protection mechanism for sensitive interfaces.
    

> Operation steps
> 
> 1.  Manually turn off input protection: Phone's built-in settings ---- Input method settings ---- Secure keyboard/Secure input
>     
> 2.  Manually turn off payment protection: Phone's built-in settings ---- Payment protection
>     
> 3.  Unsolvable situations
>     
> 4.  Android 11 or Hongmeng 2 and above versions cannot be solved for the time being due to security restrictions of the phone system.
>     

8.What to do if there is a delay between the mobile phone screen and the computer screen?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-8.what-to-do-if-there-is-a-delay-between-the-mobile-phone-screen-and-the-computer-screen)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.Try reducing the frame rate of the small screen to the minimum[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-1.try-reducing-the-frame-rate-of-the-small-screen-to-the-minimum)

![iphone box farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fi3tHp7LKwo54dVFhIQlg%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6b3797bd-11ba-42e1-ae42-435f83300723&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=10a13e1c70a8f989d6bfe8f2a1a1695c&sv=3)

9.Panda crashes Continuous Exit[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-9.panda-crashes-continuous-exit)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.Close panda open the computer C:\\Users\\Administrator (your account name) directory and delete the ".updateFiles" folder[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-1.close-panda-open-the-computer-c-users-administrator-your-account-name-directory-and-delete-the-.up)

![Box phone farm price some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FefniiaFCd6A3MQ55dsdz%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9cce39bb-d147-4dd3-8396-acb21b8c4ea7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9128e3ba3ec4bdacd7648853bfc20007&sv=3)

### 2\. Panda pause loading[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-2.-panda-pause-loading)

Uninstall the Panda software, and open the computer "C:\\Users\\Administrator (your account name)\\AppData\\Local" Delete the "xiaowei" folder, and then reinstall the Panda.

"AppData" is a hidden folder. Search Google for how to show hidden folders.

![box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FyO6rWIScMJg4Z3aeFRPu%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D32703c09-d0a4-4401-973d-5e30f614f9f2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e04f7fe640cc927d4d66c74934062d6c&sv=3)

10.What should I do if my computer fails to automatically recognize my phone after booting up or suddenly disconnects?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-10.what-should-i-do-if-my-computer-fails-to-automatically-recognize-my-phone-after-booting-up-or-sud)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.USB Selective Suspension: The system may be shutting down power to the USB ports to save power.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-1.usb-selective-suspension-the-system-may-be-shutting-down-power-to-the-usb-ports-to-save-power)

Open Control Panel > Power Options. Select your current power plan, click Change plan settings > Change advanced power settings. Expand USB settings, and disable USB selective pause. Save the settings and restart your computer.

![How to disable USB Selective Suspend via "Control Panel" ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FSRtCbjjDm3lv5HmhxXFh%252FUSB%2520selective_en.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D50e2bf7f-318d-4090-b270-e413b550f498&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0073ded60af85caea6f2c09a491762a8&sv=3)

### 2.USB Sleep Power Supply: Some motherboards completely disconnect power to USB ports after shutdown.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq#id-2.usb-sleep-power-supply-some-motherboards-completely-disconnect-power-to-usb-ports-after-shutdown)

\*Restart your computer and enter the BIOS/UEFI settings (press Del/F2 or other keys during startup). The method to open the BIOS varies depending on the motherboard; please search on Google. \*Look for options like "USB Power Delivery in Sleep Mode" or "ErP Ready" and make sure they are enabled. \*Save settings and exit

# WIFI Mode Connection | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection.md)
.

1.WIFI mode connection steps[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection#id-1.wifi-mode-connection-steps)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  The phone needs to use a USB cable to successfully project the screen
    
2.  [Refer to the USB connection tutorial and successfully connect to Panda with a USB cable](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-1.usb-connection)
    
3.  Turn on WIFI on the phone and successfully connect to the network
    
4.  The wifi connected to the phone and the network connected to the computer need to be in the same local area network
    
5.  Right-click the small screen and click \[Switch to WIFI mode\]
    
6.  After the transfer is successful, the word WIFI will be displayed in the upper left corner of the phone, as shown below
    

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FEIVGBrpwcIg2vds3NkOy%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D35e8b4cb-d2c6-4094-8516-6b17edbbf40e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=206336a98ad89d5688713efa97196285&sv=3)

some3c.com

2.How to change the USB mode to WIFI mode?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection#id-2.how-to-change-the-usb-mode-to-wifi-mode)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Right-click the small screen and a pop-up window will pop up.
    

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FsIXD7XC2Vb9d63Q2uZG9%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1d95d354-b953-4d99-9326-698ddea4e1e9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0b1b735903f8e83a203e558e46877ab4&sv=3)

some3c.com

2.Click the button to switch to WIFI mode, and the selected device will start to switch modes.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FEXPnEzpdNiBHqYOsxHDN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Daae0b5a3-b80e-40f5-92e7-083cf325d0f2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7e5a2b4eb00f8b0d20afc67b02e28c4e&sv=3)

some3c.com

1.  After the conversion is successful, the word WIFI will be displayed in the upper left corner of the small screen.
    

Note: USB to WIFI necessary conditions:

1.the mobile phone must be connected to WIFI

2\. The WIFI network connected to the mobile phone must be in the same LAN as the network of the host computer

3.How to switch all mobile phones to wifi mode in batches?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection#id-3.how-to-switch-all-mobile-phones-to-wifi-mode-in-batches)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.In Panda, press Ctrl+A to select all phones. Then click "More" on the right and click Wi-Fi mode.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FGDAsqgtBPFof1vGSa490%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dad5da03f-e07a-4b8e-a245-216d0ecd84fe&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1b0b415cbdfb29b0d87eaff463a7fd6d&sv=3)

some3c.com

4.Unable to switch to Wi-Fi mode?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection#id-4.unable-to-switch-to-wi-fi-mode)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Is the router connected to the computer the same as the router connected to your phone?
    
2.  Is the network between the computer and the phone interoperable? Follow the steps below to verify:
    
3.  Press win+R on the computer keyboard
    
4.  Enter cmd in the pop-up run window and press Enter to open the terminal window
    
5.  Enter "ping 192.168.1.112" in the terminal window (the IP address after ping is the real IP address of your phone)

**Remove system security blocks** (Frequently Asked Questions):

Because it's not available on the App Store, when you first double-click to open it on macOS,

The system may display a message saying

"**Cannot open because Apple cannot check if it contains malware**".

**Solution**:

Open "System Settings" -> "Privacy & Security" on your Mac, scroll down to find the "Security" section, click "Open Anyway," and enter your Mac password to run normally.

4\. Installation failure problem.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-4.-installation-failure-problem)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If the following prompt pops up during the installation process, it is because there is a folder permission problem when installing the Microsoft Edge Webview2 component. Simply delete the C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Microsoft folder, or change the folder name.

![xiaowei Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FlF4wZR74VWN8g2wReTSW%252F1.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9dc9b8c0-dd10-451f-b949-692c746b3802&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d28d8725d4731b0c156ec048b419fdf1&sv=3)

The official download address of WebView2 from Microsoft is:[https://developer.microsoft.com/zh-cn/microsoft-edge/webview2/#download-section](https://developer.microsoft.com/zh-cn/microsoft-edge/webview2/#download-section)

5\. Conflict reminder[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-6619619c)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When using Panda Screen Mirroring, please close other similar screen mirroring software.

Panda Screencast is stable and reliable, but when used with other similar screencasting software, there will be conflicts such as ports being occupied and programs being locked, which may cause users to experience many abnormal problems such as the inability to cast from their mobile phones, a small number of mobile phone screens being cast, and the failure of group control functions. For your user experience, please close other screencasting software.

6\. What are the compatible systems for Panda Mirror Screen Casting?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-6.-what-are-the-compatible-systems-for-panda-mirror-screen-casting)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Compatible computer system

Compatible Android version

Compatible mobile phone models

1.  Android 6 above
    

1.  Samsung series
    

1.  Lightning simulator
    

1.  Windows 10 64

When you use a non-compatible computer system or mobile phone model, some abnormalities may occur.

We are planning to add support for other phone models, give us some time and it will come later.

7.What should I do if it prompts that the software is already running?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-7.what-should-i-do-if-it-prompts-that-the-software-is-already-running)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When you open the software, it prompts "Panda is already running" because there are other programs in the system occupying port 9999, which makes it impossible to start normally. The solution is also very simple. Open the system's task manager, find the program process with port 9999 and end it, then you can open the software and use it normally

8.What should I do if it says the system has stopped running?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-8.what-should-i-do-if-it-says-the-system-has-stopped-running)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Usually, this is due to incompatibility of Windows 7 system. It is recommended to use Windows 10 or Windows 11 system.

9.What should I do if the software crashes?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/download#id-9.what-should-i-do-if-the-software-crashes)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When the user's computer was upgraded from win7 to win10, wmic had changed the original directory structure, and the environment variables did not follow the table, causing the wmic command to fail.

Solution

1.  Win+R, run sysdm.cpl
    
2.  Advanced - Environment Variables
    
3.  System Variables - Path - Edit
    
4.  Add C:\\Windows\\System32\\wbem (note the capitalization)

1.How to purchase and activate Panda Android Control Membership?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/members-and-versions#id-1.how-to-purchase-and-activate-panda-android-control-membership)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Operation steps

1.  Click the control panel version information,
    
2.  You can see the purchase membership button on this page, click to purchase membership
    

![phone farm price some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FGJ6KdGwrPM4iZnA3jRJh%252F%25E5%25BE%25AE%25E4%25BF%25A1%25E5%259B%25BE%25E7%2589%2587_20251107172409.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db5f6857d-2b82-4ffc-995c-aeab28b58402&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=80c153d318e002afd11d5f6e65043a41&sv=3)

some3c.com

Note:

● One account can be logged in on multiple computers

● One activation code can only be used on one computer. If there are two computers, you can log in with one account, but you need to purchase two activation codes

2\. What is the difference between the free version and the VIP version?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/members-and-versions#id-2.-what-is-the-difference-between-the-free-version-and-the-vip-version)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

All functions of Panda Android Screen Mirroring are permanently free. The functions of the free version and the member version are exactly the same. The only difference is the maximum number of screen mirroring devices. The details are as follows

Function

Free

Member

ADB Connection

40

No restrictions.

Accessibility Connection

5

No restrictions.

Secure Connection

2

No restrictions.

API interface

none

support

Phone Sharing

5

No restrictions.

3.Why is it not a membership version after successful purchase?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/members-and-versions#id-3.why-is-it-not-a-membership-version-after-successful-purchase)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You need to activate it manually. After completing your purchase, go to the Panda page, 1.click on your email account, 2.then go to the "Activation Code Purchase Records" page, 3.click on the activation code, and activate it manually.

![Panda Android Control](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FF3ve44rXC164LAIJ4oUD%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcd1023b5-0cc9-47aa-a2f9-6caee41effd3&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0e578b57cc15040b71aff33e71897fc7&sv=3)

4.Can the activation code be bound to a different computer?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/members-and-versions#id-4.can-the-activation-code-be-bound-to-a-different-computer)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can change the binding, and the interval between each change is 2 hours. Each activation code can support a maximum of 10 computers.

1.  The same activation code can only be activated and used on one device at the same time. Multiple devices cannot use the same activation code at the same time, but you can change the binding.
    
2.  When a new device uses the same activation code to activate a new device, the old device will be automatically squeezed out and forced to return to the free version.
    
3.  Each activation code can only be activated once every 2 hours, and each activation code can only be changed on a maximum of 10 computers.

# Accessible Mode Connection | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/accessible-mode-connection.md)
.

1\. Accessibility mode connection settings.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/accessible-mode-connection#id-1.-accessibility-mode-connection-settings)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!!

Box Phone farm motherboard users please use with caution, because barrier-free reconnection requires manual authorization on the mobile phone screen,

so mobile motherboards must not turn off developer options

1.  When using Accessibility for the first time, you must first use ADB to successfully project the screen
    
2.  There are three ADB projection methods: USB, WIF, and OTG
    
3.  After successfully projecting the screen using adb, right-click and click \[Switch to Accessibility Mode\]
    

![Panda Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F3t8w0qIaG867Q2o5WZFA%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5b1d6fbd-34fa-473b-a549-5815452c03ec&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=251fef30d5b9ea8f674d89dee79d3341&sv=3)

some3c.com

4.Click \[Confirm to Accessibility\] again on the pop-up window.

![Panda Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FoCbsqUfUXU464MybM7Ub%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3728f441-be82-4b79-92eb-bee24b3a39e6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=043f76fe56a967487fd15a97677a7bf1&sv=3)

some3c.com

5.If the \[Screen Casting Assistant\] is not installed on your phone, it will automatically install it for you. If it is already installed, just ignore it.

![Panda Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fa2kOTXsPSwco65vM31RU%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D58c526ac-9500-448c-a28f-46d5641e73cb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fdc029da4d66f1732d380b1c52f7f681&sv=3)

some3c.com

6.On your phone, click Connect to PC.

![Panda Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FZ4yApNiCS8Cb3z93ytmQ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd0ffd65d-7bc3-46cd-88bc-1f81239d29f7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7bb71f2982c93ae91cb837ecc6748f88&sv=3)

some3c.com

1.  After clicking Start Now, an authorization accessibility box will pop up. Click the Cast Assistant to enter the Cast Assistant authorization page and enable the option.
    

![Panda Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FjWzBSz3RPrieqNtng4ly%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc7c2852f-3c80-4185-9c83-4bbe6194b953&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1a623895cec856077f8cbfbecdf76ec8&sv=3)

1.  After authorization, the phone will reconnect and enter barrier-free mode. At this time, you can turn off the developer mode of the phone.

# Safe Mode Connection | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/safe-mode-connection.md)
.

1.How to switch from ADB mode to safe mode?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/safe-mode-connection#id-1.how-to-switch-from-adb-mode-to-safe-mode)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!! Important reminder!!!

Box Phone farm motherboard users please use with caution, because Safe mode reconnection requires manual authorization on the mobile phone screen,

so mobile motherboards must not turn off developer options

1.Move the mouse to More Functions, and a pop-up window will appear. Find the Safe Mode option in the More Functions pop-up window and click it.

![phone farm box android some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FpQEbvBuEYuzUF3JC5M6K%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Daa83d901-db81-4070-b84b-3c6f7c936821&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a0c8518e3ba1a1eed87a36223feef2bc&sv=3)

some3c.com

2.Click Next and manually turn off the \[USB debugging\] option on your phone.After turning off the USB debugging option, the Panda software will automatically restart.

![phone farm box android some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FzHcebnMgm5XCfkyJ4Yr7%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D91974874-11c1-422e-9373-52ea436b4873&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=458d5320893996b088058354ff801297&sv=3)

some3c.com

1.  When switching from \[ADB Mode\] to \[Safe Mode\], since each phone needs to install the USB driver one by one, the screen projection will be slower when there are many phones, so please wait patiently.
    

![phone farm apps some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fj0R5HKLcUEhyIwC9OCUG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3462084d-d3ce-4237-b1ad-a446427d87a5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=93a9f21dce699e065a2e74317037fd58&sv=3)

some3c.com

4.At this time, you need to manually click on the phone screen to authorize the USB accessory permission for \[Security Assistant\]

![phone farm apps some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FSLJj8Eo57Lz9J8NuD6Am%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4bf32e58-1679-4f19-b635-e6726fc4e53b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=083050994c07646a945b49f4703224e9&sv=3)

some3c.com

5.After following the above steps, you can achieve safe screen projection (turn off developer options)

![phone farm for sale some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FDCXSMd81KXoNduWiN5CA%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D71b3732f-c162-42d8-8611-1e14a41a68a5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=91515de7c96986d19c7290e979b1daac&sv=3)

some3c.com

> ● If the phone does not pop up the authorization box after the driver is installed, you need to change the USB charging mode to the file transfer (MTP) mode and re-plug the USB cable
> 
> ● When Panda switches to \[Safe Mode\], ADB screen projection (screen projection with USB debugging turned on) cannot be used
> 
> ● Because \[Safe Mode\] disables the ADB debugging function, related adb functions cannot be used

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)

.

1.USB Connection[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-1.usb-connection)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

USB screen projection steps

Step 1: Turn on the USB debugging mode of your phone.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FKyoMKHtqhmWxGUJeaeWg%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6baf9cf9-ff04-4f89-91ec-043cf64dc7a2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9323b67828d30bde209e3618c86bcf12&sv=3)

some3c.com

Step 2: Use a USB data cable to connect to the computer.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F3RFBUMK8lcQe1UBFd2FT%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da8198199-4172-47db-9896-73645009aec7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=13be06f9301ef9ffbfadd9e05de3ff8d&sv=3)

some3c.com

Step 3: Open \[Panda\].

2..How to turn on USB debugging mode?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-2..how-to-turn-on-usb-debugging-mode)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Different models of mobile phones have different ways to open it. Please search on Google.

Different Android system versions have slightly different ways to open USB debugging.

The following uses Samsung, which is more common in the market, as an example to explain.

Step ①

Step ②

Step ③

Step ④

Step ⑤

Go to Settings on your phone and tap "About phone"

Click "Software Information."

Click the version number 3-7 times in a row until the prompt "Developer mode is enabled" appears.

Return to the settings interface, and "Developer Options" is now displayed.

Click "Developer Options", turn on "Developer Options", and then turn on "USB debugging".

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fvv5wLiIDULjBErGipj6b%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6cf0c150-a1f9-42f4-9eb2-1683dd1b9dbb&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9a79fb4cb86d9892ded2373b85770978&sv=3)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F90ea5w7NjaYTqtwVNNL7%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcfe0bab5-8c82-4ea5-8a69-9f934ea543ae&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=993a2fbf7a1e72489546029b9733df97&sv=3)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FldqU2LmOpebA2NAfW02J%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D94211bf3-9e21-4c91-88ea-23532428a27a&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1e783153fef8f1eea3bc12cf54b3f66a&sv=3)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F8VJ2KUou1mobDzdNUnvm%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D714f4a9d-50e9-4949-b008-04f71350041d&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=030ca8225135bc67d4165c43405bd51e&sv=3)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FwPrvcPBDaqsELtNKpzaL%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df4f3185b-56d9-4761-a5c4-1653dd265b4a&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7ac9ffc40d47308b88252e74d99f9bd8&sv=3)

### 1.Enable the following options in the developer mode options.[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-1.enable-the-following-options-in-the-developer-mode-options)

\[USB debugging\] - turn on

\[Allow USB installation\] - turn on (for installing the screen projection keyboard to facilitate keyboard typing)

Xiaomi/ Redmi phones need to turn on \[USB debugging-security settings\], otherwise they can be mirrored but cannot be operated

VIVO phones need to turn on \[simulated clicks\], otherwise they can be mirrored but cannot be operated

Huawei or Honor phones need to turn on \[allow USB debugging only in charging mode\]

### 2.Change the USB charging mode to \[Transfer File\] or \[MIDI\][](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-2.change-the-usb-charging-mode-to-transfer-file-or-midi)

### 3.Use a USB data cable to connect the phone and computer[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-3.use-a-usb-data-cable-to-connect-the-phone-and-computer)

The cable must have a data transfer function, because some cables can only be used for charging

It is best to connect the cable to the USB port on the back of the computer host, which is more stable, and it is best to plug it into the USB2.0 port on the back of the host (black USB port)

3.USB mode screen projection is unstable?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-3.usb-mode-screen-projection-is-unstable)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Solution

*   The cable cannot be connected (replace with the original cable)
    
*   The tail plug of the mobile phone is damaged (replace the mobile phone device)
    
*   The USB port of the computer motherboard is not powered enough (try to use the USB2.0 port on the back of the host, or increase the power supply of the host)
    
*   If a hub is used, the hub may be underpowered (try to reduce the number of devices connected to the hub)
    

4.What should I do if USB screen projection fails?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-4.what-should-i-do-if-usb-screen-projection-fails)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Is the system Win7? If yes, it is recommended to upgrade to Win10 or install Android driver.
    
2.  Is the Android system version of the phone above 7.0? Because it is not compatible with those below 7.0, it cannot project the screen.
    
3.  What phone model is it? If it is Huawei, please follow the steps below.
    

1.  Huawei phone connection steps:
    
2.  Install Huawei Mobile Assistant on the computer
    
3.  Use a USB data cable to successfully connect the phone and Huawei Assistant
    
4.  The phone will push the Huawei driver, click to install
    
5.  After the driver is successfully installed, exit Huawei Mobile Assistant on the computer
    
6.  Replug the USB cable, the phone will pop up an authorization box, click to allow
    
7.  If it still doesn't work, please try restarting the phone
    

1.  Make sure the USB cable supports data transmission, because some USB cables can only charge, and USB cables that can only charge cannot project the screen.
    
2.  Make sure the USB cable is plugged into the USB2.0 port on the back of the computer host, because the USB port on the back of the host is more stable.
    
3.  Make sure the computer can normally recognize the mobile device connected to the USB cable. If the computer cannot recognize it normally, Panda will not recognize it even more.
    
4.  Click \[Fault Detection\] on the Effect Guard software page to see if the mobile device can be recognized. If there is a device and it is not authorized, please re-plug the USB cable or restart the phone. The authorization box will pop up on the phone page. Please click Allow. If there is no device, it means that the computer has not recognized the mobile device. Please check if there are any omissions in steps 1, 2, 3, 4, and 5.

# ADB command list | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/adb-command-list.md)
.

Use Ctrl + F shortcut to quickly find the command you need ^\_^

### 1.Functional class[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/adb-command-list#id-1.functional-class)

Name

Command

Remarks

Restart

adb reboot

Restart the phone to recovery flash mode

adb reboot recovery

Restart the phone to the bootloader interface

adb reboot bootloader

Shut down

adb shell reboot -p

Restore Device DPI

adb shell wm density reset

Modify Device DPI

adb shell wm density 320

320 (custom dpi)

View Device DPI

adb shell wm density

Restore device resolution

adb shell wm size reset

Modify device resolution

adb shell wm size 1080x1920

1080X1920 (custom resolution)

View device resolution

adb shell wm size

View device serial number

adb get-serialno

View device details list

adb devices -l

View the third-party app package name

adb shell pm list packages -3

View all package names of system applications

adb shell pm list packages -s

View all app package names

adb shell pm list packages

View memory usage

adb shell dumpsys meminfo

Clear application data and cache, package needs to be specified

adb shell pm clear cn.com.test.mobile

cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name)

Uninstall the application, you need to specify the package.

adb uninstall cn.com.test.mobile

cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name)

Uninstall the application, but keep the data, you need to specify the package

adb uninstall -k cn.com.test.mobile

cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name)

Force stop application

adb shell am force-stop cn.com.test.mobile

cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name)

Screenshot

adb shell screencap -p /sdcard/01.png

01.Png (Custom screenshot name)

Install apk

adb install d:/temp/1.apk

D:/temp/1.apk (apk absolute path)

Set battery percentage

adb shell dumpsys battery set level 100

100 (custom battery percentage)

Launch the app

adb shell am start -ncn.com.test.mobile/.ui.SplashActivity

ncn.com .Ui. SplashActivity (package name)

Check the Android system version

adb shell getprop ro.build.version.release

Check the top 10 apps that occupy memory

adb shell top -s 10

View equipment brand

adb shell getprop ro.product.brand

Turn on WiFi

adb shell svc wifi enable

Turn off WiFi

adb shell svc wifi disable

Enable pointer display

adb shell settings put system pointer\_location 1

Turn off pointer display

adb shell settings put system pointer\_location 0

Simulated click

Adb shell input tap x coordinate y coordinate

Turn off updates

adb shell pm disable-user com.wssyncmldm

Execute when the screen is black

adb shell settings put system screen\_off\_timeout 2147483647

Execute once every time the software is restarted (not in black screen state).

### 2.Button type[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/adb-command-list#id-2.button-type)

Name

Command

Remarks

Power button

adb shell input keyevent 26

Menu key

adb shell input keyevent 82

HOME key

adb shell input keyevent 3

Return key

adb shell input keyevent 4

Increase volume

adb shell input keyevent 24

Lower the volume

adb shell input keyevent 25

Mute

adb shell input keyevent 164

Play/Pause

adb shell input keyevent 85

Stop playing

adb shell input keyevent 86

Play the next song

adb shell input keyevent 87

Play the previous song

adb shell input keyevent 88

Resume playback

adb shell input keyevent 126

Pause playback

adb shell input keyevent 127

Light up the screen

adb shell input keyevent 224

Turn off the screen

adb shell input keyevent 223

Slide to unlock, swipe up gesture to unlock

adb shell input swipe 300 1000 300 500

Parameters 300, 1000, 300, and 500 respectively represent the starting point x coordinate, starting point y coordinate, ending point x coordinate, and ending point y coordinate

Enter text when the focus is on a text box

adb shell input text hello

Xiaomi MIUI ADB optimization command[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/adb-command-list#xiaomi-miui-adb-optimization-command)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

name

Order

Disable smart services

adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.systemAdSolution

Disable Xiaomi e-commerce

adb shell pm disable-user com.xiaomi.ab

Disable user feedback

adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.bugreport

Disable system toxicity

adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.analytics

Remove splash screen ads

adb shell rm -f -r /sdcard/Android/data/com.miui.systemAdSolution/files

Preventing splash screen ads from recurring

adb shell touch /sdcard/Android/data/com.miui.systemAdSolution/files

# LAN Mode Connection | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/lan-mode-connection.md)
.

1.  OTG& USB dual-mode chassis supports both USB screen projection and LAN cable screen projection
    
2.  [First set the chassis to USB connection mode](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone#id-1.usb-connection)
    
3.  Connect the computer host with a USB cable and successfully project the screen
    
4.  And use a network cable to connect the chassis to the router, which must be the same router as the computer..
    
5.  Select all phones Find the Panda Classic Edition "Open Wireless Adb"
    

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FJUkKus7YqxWS9Zi7qhZr%252Fc4663e354fe498b7fbac36ead66bf4d1.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D70742bff-7773-447a-9858-d12ee879c358&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e6e603c8e31d365c8f0d016c7e0c03b4&sv=3)

1.  After clicking "Open wireless Adb", manually press double-click. (A6/A8 chassis only requires a click)the chassis button
    

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FKFoB4uk2pnyI8C3FY6GX%252F%25E6%258C%2589%25E9%2594%25AE.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D084260ea-9f18-4d79-921d-72c87f91e1ac&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=477dad46373997cd4d2670d185b003ca&sv=3)

7.Then click 'Scan WiFi', enter the local IP range, port 5555, click save, then click scan

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FZNKSlKZ7OPpVWgJC7v11%252Faa3e898b14bfb2f600f5a1647ebaad78.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3fd78ae0-40ed-432b-9a57-860d75fd5870&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=204a5107af408bd0151f84f91e2014ef&sv=3)
