# API Documentation | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation.md)
.

WebSocket API is a common and universal API interface of HTTP protocol, supporting various language calls, such as: key wizard, easy language, C++, Python, Html, Vue, Node.js and so on.

*   Panda Classic WebSocket API address: ws://127.0.0.1:33332
    
*   WebSocket Online Test: https://wstool.js.org/
    

1.Obtain all device information[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-1.obtain-all-device-information)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    { "action":"list" }

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": [\
            {\
                "deviceId": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm",\
                "name": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm",\
                "no": 1,\
                "groupNames": ["group1", "group2"]\
            },\
            {\
                "deviceId": "VBJDU18B14001987",\
                "name": "VBJDU18B14001987",\
                "no": 24,\
                "groupNames": ["group1"]\
            }\
        ]
    }

2.Screenshot[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-2.screenshot)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    {
        "action": "screen",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "savePath": "d:/quickmirror",
            "onlyDeviceName": 1,
            "customName": "test"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    
*   onlyDeviceName: Whether to directly use the device serial number as the screenshot file name
    
    *   1 means that the screenshot file name only has the device serial number (for example `ga7d65ylnj6xinhm.png` )
        
    *   0 indicates that the screenshot file name contains the device number and screenshot time (for example `1_ga7d65ylnj6xinhm_20240707_114500_096 .png` )
        
    
*   customName: Specify the name used for the screenshot (no suffix is required), if this parameter is not empty, the name will be used as the screenshot name first, and customName can only be set when there is only one device in deviceIds
    
*   Note: If it is a wireless connection, the `:` and `.` in the device serial number will be replaced by `_` (for example, the device `192.168.2.109:5555` screenshot file name is `1_192_168_2_109_5555_20240707_114919_656 .png` )
    

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "OK"
    }

3.Screen control[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-3.screen-control)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    {
        "action": "PointerEvent",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "mask": "0",
            "x": "0.5",
            "y": "0.5",
            "endx": "0",
            "endy": "0",
            "delta": "0"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    
*   Mask: Event type: 0 press, 1 move, 2 release, 3 right mouse button (return), 4 scroll up, 5 scroll down, 6 slide up, 7 slide down, 8 slide left, 9 slide right
    
*   X, y: event coordinates, pass percentage, range is 0-1
    
*   Endx, endy: End coordinates. These two parameters are only needed for up, down, left, and right sliding events. Other events do not need to pass them
    
*   Delta: The amplitude of the scroll wheel event, the range is an integer greater than 0, the larger the value, the greater the scroll wheel rolling amplitude, only the scroll wheel event needs this parameter, other events do not need to pass
    

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "OK"
    }

Example: Swipe up screen

Copy

    //First, execute the command: mask=0, x=0.5, y=0.5, starting from the center.
    {
        "action": "PointerEvent",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "mask": "0",
            "x": "0.5",
            "y": "0.5",
            "endx": "0",
            "endy": "0",
            "delta": "0"
        }
    }
    //Execute "move mask=1" to move to the 0.2 position. You can execute multiple "move" commands before releasing the button; doing so will result in dragging.  
    {
        "action": "PointerEvent",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "mask": "1",
            "x": "0.5",
            "y": "0.2",
            "endx": "0",
            "endy": "0",
            "delta": "0"
        }
    }
    //Finally, release `mask=2 x y` and pass the final position.
    {
        "action": "PointerEvent",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "mask": "2",
            "x": "0.5",
            "y": "0.2",
            "endx": "0",
            "endy": "0",
            "delta": "0"
        }
    }

4.Get clipboard content[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-4.get-clipboard-content)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    {
        "action": "getclipboard",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "8318fc66"
        }
    }

*   DeviceIds: This command does not support multiple phone deviceIds
    

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "Shear Plate Test"
    }

5.Send to clipboard[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-5.send-to-clipboard)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    {
        "action": "writeclipboard",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "all",
            "content": "Hi,panda"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "OK"
    }

6.Execute ADB commands[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-6.execute-adb-commands)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request:

Copy

    {
        "action": "adb",
        "comm": {
            "command": "am start -a android.intent.action.VIEW -d https://some3c.com",
            "deviceIds": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    

Response:

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": {
            "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm": [\
                "Starting: Intent { act=android.intent.action.VIEW dat=https://some3c.com/... }"\
            ]
        }
    }

7.Execute ADB bat[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-7.execute-adb-bat)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request： Execute custom bat：

Copy

    {
        "action": "ExecuteBat",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm",
            "filePath" :"C:\\Scripts\\test.bat"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    

Response：

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "OK"
    }

8.Execute AutoX.js script[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/api-documentation#id-8.execute-autox.js-script)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Request： Execute custom AutoX.js：

Copy

    {
        "action": "ExecuteAutoJs",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm",
            "filePath" :"C:\\Scripts\\test.js"
        }
    }

Stop custom AutoX.js：

Copy

    {
        "action": "StopAutoJs",
        "comm": {
            "deviceIds": "ga7d65ylnj6xinhm",
            "filePath" :"C:\\Scripts\\test.js"
        }
    }

*   deviceIds: `"all"` means all devices, or devices can be specified by passing a comma-separated deviceId, for example `"ga7d65ylnj6xinhm, VBJDU18B14001987"`
    

Response：

Copy

    {
        "StatusCode": 200,
        "result": "OK"
    }

# Configuration related | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/configuration-related.md)
.

1.Get a list of connected hardware[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/configuration-related#id-1.get-a-list-of-connected-hardware)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/configuration-related#diao-yong-shuo-ming)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/config/usb/get

Request function name

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/config/usb/get'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/config/usb/get

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '{
        "fun": "/config/usb/get",
        "data": {
        }
    }'

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/configuration-related#xiang-ying-jie-guo)

Copy

     {
      "data": {
        "list": [\
          {\
            "vid": "720D",\
            "pid": "1F2D",\
            "uid": "1F2D24BD",\
            "ver": "4.4",\
            "state": 2\
          },\
          {\
            "vid": "720D",\
            "pid": "FF2D",\
            "uid": "FF2D24BD",\
            "ver": "4.4",\
            "state": 2\
          }\
        ],
        "code": 0,
        "id": "",
        "message": "成功"

\-

Boolean

Enable hardware decoding

*   Only the following settings do not require a kernel restart: \[Console language, screen mirroring discovery rules, number of retries for connection failure, allowed IP list, automatic connection and screen mirroring, automatic upgrade, use thread mode for batch hardware operations, use fast and precise mouse movement, and use the mode of rotating the screen to the right in landscape mode\]. All others require a kernel restart to take effect.
    
*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/config/imserver/set?air_play_name=iMouseXP-D2B6&lang=%E7%AE%80%E4%BD%93%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87&mdns_type=0&connect_failed_retry=2&air_play_ratio=1080&opencv_num=1&ocr_num=1&allow_ip_list=[]&air_play_fps=30&air_play_img_fps=30&air_play_refresh_rate=60&air_play_port=17000&air_play_audio=true&auto_connect=true&auto_updata=true&thread_mode=true&flip_right=true&auto_updata=true&mouse_mode=true'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/config/imserver/set \
      --form air_play_name=iMouseXP-D2B6 \
      --form lang=%E7%AE%80%E4%BD%93%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87 \
      --form mdns_type=0 \
      --form connect_failed_retry=1 \
      --form air_play_ratio=0 \
      --form opencv_num=1 \
      --form ocr_num=1 \
      --form air_play_fps=30 \
      --form air_play_img_fps=30 \
      --form air_play_refresh_rate=60 \
      --form air_play_port=17000 \
      --form air_play_audio=true \
      --form auto_connect=true \
      --form auto_updata=true \
      --form mouse_mode=true \
      --form thread_mode=true \
      --form flip_right=true 

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**

    }'

# Build Setup Mobile Farm Guide | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide.md)
.

What needs to be prepared.[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#what-needs-to-be-prepared)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.Phone Plan:[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-1.phone-plan)

Select a sufficient number of Android phones such as Samsung, Xiaomi, Huawei, Redmi, VIVO, etc Android 6.0 and above. from the second-hand market or other channels according to your needs. (Contains battery)

![what is box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FexDgpaheqOaRFLB8wxdJ%252Fe60bb40ed6ba528515a42421544e528.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddd17aeb1-e302-4f10-b59f-74a65f1ad0ae&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=82d15d3ec24f5f26724cf09a41b21015&sv=3)

some3c.com

### 2.Requirement[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-2.requirement)

It is best to use the same model, including the screen or prepare a screen to enable USB debugging mode, or the motherboard of the mobile phone, set it up to automatically start when powered on (Remove the battery and switch to powering the motherboard)

![setup box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FB0nFClVhrgI6obvN6gBH%252F%25E7%259B%25B4%25E4%25BE%259B%25E7%25BA%25BF2.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd738a03c-17c8-46c4-9811-d3941f90716a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b7098ef1e80ad87b5987ba0629ecb7a1&sv=3)

some3c.com

Not all models support power supply from the motherboard power cord

If it is not in the list, you can inquire whether it supports customization.

### 3.**USB HUB Board**[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-3.usb-hub-board)

**Used to connect all mobile phones to the computer. (Standard 20 or 21 USB ports)**

**Software Package download**

[A20.rar](https://1443965649-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%2Fuploads%2FhuxZwzy2ZxbejuoLivyo%2FA20.rar?alt=media&token=2594b167-5ae1-41fd-9e39-bca1aa270c85)

617KB

Download[Open](https://1443965649-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%2Fuploads%2FhuxZwzy2ZxbejuoLivyo%2FA20.rar?alt=media&token=2594b167-5ae1-41fd-9e39-bca1aa270c85)

![phone farming some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FbkteLxWRyz2hAW6HXES1%252FBalckusb.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2806cbd7-0351-46ee-b45a-b3148eb2c5be&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6128e4e28b26d406c16c9e8dfe09d8ee&sv=3)

some3c.com

### **4.Power adapter**[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-4.power-adapter)

**Provide stable power for all mobile phones (5V/40A or 5V/60A).**

![phone farming some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F4eI9GLefXL8StjKkAG9G%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df2b1fa96-4104-4008-9af4-3f35cb2fa2db&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=06990dc7e18878db00c149d1f222af46&sv=3)

![phone farming some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F72EU57gghcsqkZZFhddi%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcf75581d-22fe-43bd-8fe5-62d8c34d15b7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d5f908ebdbfe23e708fff54a340762e2&sv=3)

![phone farming some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FbZx9jngo9zqktytkH5SW%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D62f96e02-4308-4ae6-9981-d4b127bb7576&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2afdb7d18999e628a01b36d3a0066ca4&sv=3)

### 7.Chassis and Dock HUB[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-7.chassis-and-dock-hub)

Place the mobile phone and keep it tidy. Save space " [Click to view](https://www.some3c.com/collections/phonefarm) "

Chassis (power supply & data separation)

![Suitable for DIY](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FzCO1D1srvdP3Za8Z1xO5%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D549b3da9-3010-429b-8ccf-5adf7a8fa921&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f9f9e66a6faf43cf1927adec5d0e1019&sv=3)

![Works on complete mobile phones](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FjamfoAk9eAGpqmCIJ32q%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D66f52a45-4877-4813-97c8-0e1800938dda&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b1e48eecf498439a0c3fa175e5cc787b&sv=3)

### 8.Mirror Android phone screen to computer[](https://doc.some3c.com/user-guide/build-setup-mobile-farm-guide#id-8.mirror-android-phone-screen-to-computer)

After receiving the box, remember not to plug it into the computer first.

After setting the following steps PC , Then connect the power supply and plug it into the computer USB port.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/wifi-mode-connection.md). # WIFI Mode Connection ## 1.WIFI mode connection steps 1. The phone needs to use a USB cable to successfully project the screen 2. \[Refer to the USB connection tutorial and successfully connect to Panda with a USB cable\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/pages/Ta8WFt0VjRJp0rhTislN#id-1.usb-connection) 3. Turn on WIFI on the phone and successfully connect to the network 4. The wifi connected to the phone and the network connected to the computer need to be in the same local area network 5. Right-click the small screen and click \\\[Switch to WIFI mode\] 6. After the transfer is successful, the word WIFI will be displayed in the upper left corner of the phone, as shown below

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/2sYtAfDcVFvOwjUzYv90)

some3c.com

\## 2.How to change the USB mode to WIFI mode? 1. Right-click the small screen and a pop-up window will pop up.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/b4vIRNRmLUBk4E1Y9dYu)

some3c.com

2.Click the button to switch to WIFI mode, and the selected device will start to switch modes.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/HpmTDWJyOZX8y1MHOJm8)

some3c.com

3\. After the conversion is successful, the word WIFI will be displayed in the upper left corner of the small screen. {% hint style="info" %} Note: USB to WIFI necessary conditions: 1.the mobile phone must be connected to WIFI 2\. The WIFI network connected to the mobile phone must be in the same LAN as the network of the host computer {% endhint %} ## 3.How to switch all mobile phones to wifi mode in batches? 1.In Panda, press Ctrl+A to select all phones. Then click "More" on the right and click Wi-Fi mode.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/dEr4VlkB069xxfBEeivn)

some3c.com

\## 4.Unable to switch to Wi-Fi mode? 1. Is the router connected to the computer the same as the router connected to your phone? 2. Is the network between the computer and the phone interoperable? Follow the steps below to verify: 3. Press win+R on the computer keyboard 4. Enter cmd in the pop-up run window and press Enter to open the terminal window 5. Enter "ping 192.168.1.112" in the terminal window (the IP address after ping is the real IP address of your phone)

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/members-and-versions.md). # Members and Versions ## 1.How to purchase and activate Panda Android Control Membership? Operation steps 1. Click the control panel version information, 2. You can see the purchase membership button on this page, click to purchase membership

![phone farm price some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/4Fb1V9pxUZoqAUsaqTz4)

some3c.com

{% hint style="info" %} Note: ● One account can be logged in on multiple computers ● One activation code can only be used on one computer. If there are two computers, you can log in with one account, but you need to purchase two activation codes {% endhint %} ## 2. What is the difference between the free version and the VIP version? All functions of Panda Android Screen Mirroring are permanently free. The functions of the free version and the member version are exactly the same. The only difference is the maximum number of screen mirroring devices. The details are as follows | Function | Free | Member | | :----------------------: | :--: | :--------------: | | ADB Connection | 40 | No restrictions. | | Accessibility Connection | 5 | No restrictions. | | Secure Connection | 2 | No restrictions. | | API interface | none | support | | Phone Sharing | 5 | No restrictions. | ## 3.Why is it not a membership version after successful purchase? You need to activate it manually. After completing your purchase, go to the Panda page, 1.click on your email account, 2.then go to the "Activation Code Purchase Records" page, 3.click on the activation code, and activate it manually.

![Panda Android Control](https://doc.some3c.com/files/gSBrTpXXbcP0mpKiGHYR)

\## 4.Can the activation code be bound to a different computer? You can change the binding, and the interval between each change is 2 hours. Each activation code can support a maximum of 10 computers. 1. The same activation code can only be activated and used on one device at the same time. Multiple devices cannot use the same activation code at the same time, but you can change the binding. 2. When a new device uses the same activation code to activate a new device, the old device will be automatically squeezed out and forced to return to the free version. 3. Each activation code can only be activated once every 2 hours, and each activation code can only be changed on a maximum of 10 computers.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart.md). # Panda Android Screen Projection ### 1.Product interface

![xiaowei Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/NhkfwyELIuIEyhEIzQXw)

some3c.com

Panda Screen Mirroring is a software used to connect and manage Android phones on a computer. It is specially designed for batch screen mirroring management of Android phones. It can greatly improve the operating efficiency of managing Android phones on a computer, especially in batch operations, automated operations, In terms of remote operation, efficiency has been significantly improved. ### 2. Application scenarios [](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart.md#id-3dbf0c11)
Panda Screencast is a connection tool that uses a USB cable or other connection methods to project the Android phone screen onto the computer screen through video streaming. The computer mouse is used instead of fingers to slide and click on the phone screen. The purpose is to facilitate mobile application developers to view and operate mobile application software on the computer. The connection between Android phone and computer is the connection method that comes with the Android system. Panda Screencast only plays a connecting role and does not make any modifications or damage to the mobile phone system, nor does it provide any auxiliary functions for any mobile application. It only provides the most basic connection function between Android and Windows. ### 3. Multi-language support[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/quickstart.md#id-8b25bff8)

![xiaowei Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/uRU9OcdspPkDfcm4cnLx)

some3c.com

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/connection-faq.md). # Connection FAQ ## 1.Xiaomi Redmi is out of control? Xiaomi Redmi series phones need to turn on \\\[USB debugging\] \\\[USB installation\] \\\[USB debugging security settings\] to control

![phone farm for sale some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/UzNusYHlQ6zvmrGEpHVO)

some3c.com

\## 2.VIVO phone cannot be controlled? VIVO series phones need to turn on \\\[USB debugging\] \\\[USB simulated click\] settings to control

![phone farm setup some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/TYxHJpBhs2ZG82ijkjRJ)

some3c.com

And turn on "USB Safety Permissions", then you enable USB debugging successfully

![phone farm setup some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/rxyRYnuvc7j0kqOLckj4)

\## 3.Huawei Honor phone cannot be recognized? Huawei or Honor phones need to download the corresponding mobile assistant on their official websites (ignore for other brands of phones) Huawei Mobile Assistant: ; Honor Mobile Assistant: 1\. Install the mobile assistant on your computer 2. Use a USB data cable to successfully connect the phone and assistant (the mobile assistant has a connection tutorial) 3. The assistant will push the Huawei or Honor mobile phone driver to the phone, click to install 4. After the driver is successfully installed, turn off the mobile assistant and re-plug the USB cable 5. If it still doesn't work, try restarting the phone ## 4. Computer Problems ### 1. What is the upper limit of device connection? Panda Android Mirror Screen Casting Software itself has no limit on the number of connected mobile phones. The number of connected devices is determined by the user's computer motherboard or router.

![Phone farm software some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/1clXZcZ59dyqP1WLyHHw)

some3c.com

USB connection mode 1. Theoretically, the motherboard of an ordinary home computer host supports a maximum of 127 USB connections, but most motherboard manufacturers limit the number of USB connections to 40. Therefore, under normal circumstances, a home computer host can only connect to 40 devices. 2. The motherboard of a commercial server usually adopts the X79 or X99 architecture, which has no limit on the number of USB connections and can be unlimited in theory. OTG or WIFI connection mode 1. If you use network connection modes such as OTG or WIFI, the number of connections is not limited by the computer motherboard, so there is no need to consider the problem of the computer motherboard. But it is limited by the performance of the router. 2. Usually, home routers can only support up to 20 devices for stable use, and more will cause frequent disconnection. Enterprise routers can usually support up to 40 devices for stable use, and more will cause frequent disconnection. 3. The most cost-effective router solution is soft routing. In theory, a soft routing device can support hundreds of devices for stable use at the same time. Of course, the premise is that your bandwidth must be sufficient. If the upstream and downstream speeds of the bandwidth are not enough, it will still be stuck. ### 2. What if the device cannot be detected? First, check whether the computer (open this computer) recognizes the device. If the computer does not recognize the device, the software cannot recognize it, so you need to find the problem with the computer. If there is a device in the computer and the software cannot recognize it, there are probably the following reasons Cause analysis: 1. The mobile phone USB debugging switch is not turned on; 2. The device has not installed the mobile phone driver; 3. Adb conflict, there are multiple different versions of Adb.exe files in the system. Solution: 4. Check USB debugging in the mobile phone developer options. 5. Refer to the mobile phone driver installation problem to install the driver. 6. Refer to the Adb conflict resolution method. ### 3. What to do if USB controller resources are insufficient?

![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/yXXdIGrktuUOqgkWrLm6)

some3c.com

First turn off the \\\[XHCI\] item under \\\[USB\] in \\\[BIOS\], then try to change the USB port of the computer, and finally try to use a specific USB expansion card. If some motherboards have extra card slots, you can buy a USB expansion card. One expansion card can carry about 15 devices. It depends on the computer motherboard. ### 4.What should I do if the Panda device is detected and it says it is not authorized? 1. Find a merchant to obtain the adb authorization file (adbkey, adbkey.pub), 2. Put these two files in the C:\\Users\\Administrator.android folder, overwrite them, and then restart the computer.

![Phone farm software some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/E8shjpIU8BTBE4IA0lGt)

\## 5.How to check device status using the device list? Click on the panel "Devices" to view the status of all devices.

![iphone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/6RlQCigOByQEzCmzXwrn)

\## 6.What should I do if it prompts that the device is disconnected from the server?

![iphone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/QklJ00WwSTpBx2NN0XOh)

{% hint style="info" %} \*\*Common reasons\*\* The user's local computer has incorrect time or time zone, which will cause disconnection with the server \*\*Uncommon reasons\*\* The user has opened a VPN, which causes the connection delay with Panda to be too high. The user has opened a proxy server in the computer settings. The user has opened a packet capture tool, which causes Panda to fail to connect. \*\*Solution\*\* 1.Change the computer local time to the current time\\ 2.Exit VPN, IP proxy tool, and then restart Panda,\\ 3.Exit the packet capture tool (such as Wireshark, Fiddler, etc.)\\ 4.Reconnect your computer to the Internet. {% endhint %} ## 7.What should I do if the screen goes black or the connection is disconnected when using a certain type of app? {% hint style="info" %} Problem 1. The phone screen goes black when paying 2. The phone screen goes black when entering the password Reason 1. The phone system's security protection mechanism for sensitive interfaces. {% endhint %} > Operation steps > > 1. Manually turn off input protection: Phone's built-in settings ---- Input method settings ---- Secure keyboard/Secure input > 2. Manually turn off payment protection: Phone's built-in settings ---- Payment protection > 3. Unsolvable situations > 4. Android 11 or Hongmeng 2 and above versions cannot be solved for the time being due to security restrictions of the phone system. ## 8.What to do if there is a delay between the mobile phone screen and the computer screen? ### 1.Try reducing the frame rate of the small screen to the minimum

![iphone box farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/Gx92hPoiz8R7kAyM3pYN)

\### 2.Check whether the \\\[Random Delay\] switch is turned on. If it is turned on, please turn it off.

![iphone box farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/04ZFq0F6IanLRhnzychs)

\### 3.Check whether the \\\[Synchronization Delay\] switch is turned on. If it is turned on, turn it off.

![Box phone farm price some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/tTmEpcSx6YfQdWBCPhpE)

\### 4.It is recommended to replace a computer with a higher configuration. The more CPU cores there are, the smoother the operation will be. It is recommended to try to update the data cable. The data transmission rate of the data cable may be too low. ## 9.Panda crashes Continuous Exit ### 1.Close panda open the computer C:\\Users\\Administrator (your account name) directory and delete the ".updateFiles" folder

![Box phone farm price some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/8I3gbocdIv6pzAO6NEKl)

\### 2. Panda pause loading Uninstall the Panda software, and open the computer "C:\\Users\\Administrator (your account name)\\AppData\\Local" Delete the "xiaowei" folder, and then reinstall the Panda. {% hint style="warning" %} "AppData" is a hidden folder. Search Google for how to show hidden folders. {% endhint %}

![box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/files/eg38yOU2ZJhPbbeU4QcO)

\## 10.What should I do if my computer fails to automatically recognize my phone after booting up or suddenly disconnects? ### 1.USB Selective Suspension: The system may be shutting down power to the USB ports to save power. Open Control Panel > Power Options. Select your current power plan, click Change plan settings > Change advanced power settings. Expand USB settings, and disable USB selective pause. Save the settings and restart your computer.

![How to disable USB Selective Suspend via "Control Panel" ](https://doc.some3c.com/files/ff5JbJXWXgEtypvYmedo)

\### 2.USB Sleep Power Supply: Some motherboards completely disconnect power to USB ports after shutdown. \\\*Restart your computer and enter the BIOS/UEFI settings (press Del/F2 or other keys during startup). The method to open the BIOS varies depending on the motherboard; please search on Google.\\ \\\*Look for options like "USB Power Delivery in Sleep Mode" or "ErP Ready" and make sure they are enabled.\\ \\\*Save settings and exit

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/usb-connect-phone.md). # USB Connect Phone ## 1.USB Connection USB screen projection steps Step 1: Turn on the USB debugging mode of your phone.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/OGNXcGeQM9Yb17w0hDrk)

some3c.com

Step 2: Use a USB data cable to connect to the computer.

![LAIXI Android Screen Projection](https://doc.some3c.com/files/gf9yw6jNDbT5MKJhjl91)

some3c.com

Step 3: Open \\\[Panda\]. ## 2..How to turn on USB debugging mode? Different models of mobile phones have different ways to open it. Please search on Google. Different Android system versions have slightly different ways to open USB debugging. The following uses Samsung, which is more common in the market, as an example to explain.

|     |     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Step ① | Step ② | Step ③ | Step ④ | Step ⑤ |
| Go to Settings on your phone and tap "About phone" | Click "Software Information." | Click the version number 3-7 times in a row until the prompt "Developer mode is enabled" appears. | Return to the settings interface, and "Developer Options" is now displayed. | Click "Developer Options", turn on "Developer Options", and then turn on "USB debugging". |
| ![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/X2r3I2Lb4yRSo9vgh6Z1) | ![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/cz6LHb7fpC2SXd2KDNys) | ![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/jqdKwBQotW4YHgEdlqcp) | ![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/CsmSuW1G2UjTN2lqlHb1) | ![](https://doc.some3c.com/files/NXD4WkkkMrx7wV8xI993) |

\### 1.Enable the following options in the developer mode options. \\\[USB debugging\] - turn on \\\[Allow USB installation\] - turn on (for installing the screen projection keyboard to facilitate keyboard typing) Xiaomi/ Redmi phones need to turn on \\\[USB debugging-security settings\], otherwise they can be mirrored but cannot be operated VIVO phones need to turn on \\\[simulated clicks\], otherwise they can be mirrored but cannot be operated Huawei or Honor phones need to turn on \\\[allow USB debugging only in charging mode\] ### 2.Change the USB charging mode to \\\[Transfer File\] or \\\[MIDI\] ### 3.Use a USB data cable to connect the phone and computer The cable must have a data transfer function, because some cables can only be used for charging It is best to connect the cable to the USB port on the back of the computer host, which is more stable, and it is best to plug it into the USB2.0 port on the back of the host (black USB port) ## 3.USB mode screen projection is unstable? Solution \* The cable cannot be connected (replace with the original cable) \* The tail plug of the mobile phone is damaged (replace the mobile phone device) \* The USB port of the computer motherboard is not powered enough (try to use the USB2.0 port on the back of the host, or increase the power supply of the host) \* If a hub is used, the hub may be underpowered (try to reduce the number of devices connected to the hub) ## 4.What should I do if USB screen projection fails? 1. Is the system Win7? If yes, it is recommended to upgrade to Win10 or install Android driver. 2. Is the Android system version of the phone above 7.0? Because it is not compatible with those below 7.0, it cannot project the screen. 3. What phone model is it? If it is Huawei, please follow the steps below. {% hint style="info" %} 1. Huawei phone connection steps: 2. Install Huawei Mobile Assistant on the computer 3. Use a USB data cable to successfully connect the phone and Huawei Assistant 4. The phone will push the Huawei driver, click to install 5. After the driver is successfully installed, exit Huawei Mobile Assistant on the computer 6. Replug the USB cable, the phone will pop up an authorization box, click to allow 7. If it still doesn't work, please try restarting the phone {% endhint %} 8) Make sure the USB cable supports data transmission, because some USB cables can only charge, and USB cables that can only charge cannot project the screen. 9) Make sure the USB cable is plugged into the USB2.0 port on the back of the computer host, because the USB port on the back of the host is more stable. 10) Make sure the computer can normally recognize the mobile device connected to the USB cable. If the computer cannot recognize it normally, Panda will not recognize it even more. 11) Click \\\[Fault Detection\] on the Effect Guard software page to see if the mobile device can be recognized. If there is a device and it is not authorized, please re-plug the USB cable or restart the phone. The authorization box will pop up on the phone page. Please click Allow. If there is no device, it means that the computer has not recognized the mobile device. Please check if there are any omissions in steps 1, 2, 3, 4, and 5.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/adb-command-list.md). # ADB command list Use Ctrl + F shortcut to quickly find the command you need ^\\\_^ ### 1.Functional class | Name | Command | Remarks | | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | | Restart | adb reboot |

  

| | Restart the phone to recovery flash mode | adb reboot recovery |

  

| | Restart the phone to the bootloader interface | adb reboot bootloader |

  

| | Shut down | adb shell reboot -p |

  

| | Restore Device DPI | adb shell wm density reset |

  

| | Modify Device DPI | adb shell wm density 320 | 320 (custom dpi) | | View Device DPI | adb shell wm density |

  

| | Restore device resolution | adb shell wm size reset |

  

| | Modify device resolution | adb shell wm size 1080x1920 | 1080X1920 (custom resolution) | | View device resolution | adb shell wm size |

  

| | View device serial number | adb get-serialno |

  

| | View device details list | adb devices -l |

  

| | View the third-party app package name | adb shell pm list packages -3 |

  

| | View all package names of system applications | adb shell pm list packages -s |

  

| | View all app package names | adb shell pm list packages |

  

| | View memory usage | adb shell dumpsys meminfo |

  

| | Clear application data and cache, package needs to be specified | adb shell pm clear cn.com.test.mobile | cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name) | | Uninstall the application, you need to specify the package. | adb uninstall cn.com.test.mobile | cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name) | | Uninstall the application, but keep the data, you need to specify the package | adb uninstall -k cn.com.test.mobile | cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name) | | Force stop application | adb shell am force-stop cn.com.test.mobile | cn.com . test.mobile (Custom package name) | | Screenshot | adb shell screencap -p /sdcard/01.png | 01.Png (Custom screenshot name) | | Install apk | adb install d:/temp/1.apk | D:/temp/1.apk (apk absolute path) | | Set battery percentage | adb shell dumpsys battery set level 100 | 100 (custom battery percentage) | | Launch the app | adb shell am start -ncn.com.test.mobile/.ui.SplashActivity | ncn.com .Ui. SplashActivity (package name) | | Check the Android system version | adb shell getprop ro.build.version.release |

  

| | Check the top 10 apps that occupy memory | adb shell top -s 10 |

  

| | View equipment brand | adb shell getprop ro.product.brand |

  

| | Turn on WiFi | adb shell svc wifi enable |

  

| | Turn off WiFi | adb shell svc wifi disable |

  

| | Enable pointer display | adb shell settings put system pointer\\\_location 1 |

  

| | Turn off pointer display | adb shell settings put system pointer\\\_location 0 |

  

| | Simulated click | Adb shell input tap x coordinate y coordinate |

  

| | Turn off updates | adb shell pm disable-user com.wssyncmldm |

  

| | Execute when the screen is black | adb shell settings put system screen\\\_off\\\_timeout 2147483647 | Execute once every time the software is restarted (not in black screen state). | ### 2.Button type | Name | Command | Remarks | | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | | Power button | adb shell input keyevent 26 |

  

| | Menu key | adb shell input keyevent 82 |

  

| | HOME key | adb shell input keyevent 3 |

  

| | Return key | adb shell input keyevent 4 |

  

| | Increase volume | adb shell input keyevent 24 |

  

| | Lower the volume | adb shell input keyevent 25 |

  

| | Mute | adb shell input keyevent 164 |

  

| | Play/Pause | adb shell input keyevent 85 |

  

| | Stop playing | adb shell input keyevent 86 |

  

| | Play the next song | adb shell input keyevent 87 |

  

| | Play the previous song | adb shell input keyevent 88 |

  

| | Resume playback | adb shell input keyevent 126 |

  

| | Pause playback | adb shell input keyevent 127 |

  

| | Light up the screen | adb shell input keyevent 224 |

  

| | Turn off the screen | adb shell input keyevent 223 |

  

| | Slide to unlock, swipe up gesture to unlock | adb shell input swipe 300 1000 300 500 | Parameters 300, 1000, 300, and 500 respectively represent the starting point x coordinate, starting point y coordinate, ending point x coordinate, and ending point y coordinate | | Enter text when the focus is on a text box | adb shell input text hello |

  

| ## Xiaomi MIUI ADB optimization command | name | Order | | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | | Disable smart services | adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.systemAdSolution | | Disable Xiaomi e-commerce | adb shell pm disable-user com.xiaomi.ab | | Disable user feedback | adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.bugreport | | Disable system toxicity | adb shell pm disable-user com.miui.analytics | | Remove splash screen ads | adb shell rm -f -r /sdcard/Android/data/com.miui.systemAdSolution/files | | Preventing splash screen ads from recurring | adb shell touch /sdcard/Android/data/com.miui.systemAdSolution/files |
