# Panda Setting & Use | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use.md)
.

1.How to switch the interface to dark mode?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-1.how-to-switch-the-interface-to-dark-mode)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Place the mouse on the control panel icon and left-click to switch the theme of the entire Panda Android screen projection interface.

![box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FOH3WEnWoSy7jEtQZNDKZ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De29b80fc-fa6d-495c-abb6-35094c8fc239&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4a4ffc80645a7cf748eb1e5bdb5e2f9e&sv=3)

some3c.com

2.How do I switch theme colors?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-2.how-do-i-switch-theme-colors)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click System Settings, find the theme color option in the settings pop-up window, and click the color block with your mouse to select the theme color.

![box phone farm Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FBe63sjikbmd3Be1Ws3WB%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4b483e7e-c9fd-403c-9e27-c2f15039a43d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c1ace843d7b10f670f0140a45089a72f&sv=3)

some3c.com

3.How to fold and secure the control panel?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-3.how-to-fold-and-secure-the-control-panel)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Put the mouse on the control panel and left-click to collapse the panel. If you want to fix the panel, move the mouse to the rightmost side of the software and the control panel will appear. At this time, click with the mouse to fix the control panel.

![box phone farm Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FOLrQ760YMc60Fc2nPDWB%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc322ac21-f698-432a-9b0a-e8e69dcfbf5d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=09978d2fa174a6038c7a3f34b559b80f&sv=3)

some3c.com

4.How do I switch the control panel to the left?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-4.how-do-i-switch-the-control-panel-to-the-left)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click System Settings. Find the Control Panel Position option in the Settings pop-up window and click Left or Right. The control panel will switch left and right.

![box phone farm Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FD9jG8hHJ0XoFrmFGl9ii%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D74118d58-4b1b-4679-aa0d-de5cbbd5badb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d8ef526c888887183fdc79f42f59cfb0&sv=3)

some3c.com

5.How to control single and batch?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-5.how-to-control-single-and-batch)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Individual control

Batch control

Click the magnifying glass of the small window, or double-click a separate window to control it separately.

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F0fzQUkEvn8PofvRcqM6n%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddbcb3008-b359-4b12-9559-7db4874bfbd2&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b5ac519a58a61087e04b90f4066296a4&sv=3)

Click the magnifying glass of the small winUse the mouse to select some or all of them, then double-click or click the magnifying glass in one of the windows to control the batch. ![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FvEAMiZ9Sh439O38ufVZc%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3eafc891-dd2d-44f9-9036-7708c179b943&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8aeb5259d9e3d6e734e81c9c3dfc9204&sv=3)

6.How to hide the magnifying glass button on small screens[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-6.how-to-hide-the-magnifying-glass-button-on-small-screens)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click System Settings. Find the small screen magnifier option in the settings pop-up window. Click it to show or hide it.

![box phone farm kits some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FaXNsbeoFA41h17qKRxSs%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8bad9907-833f-45ef-88bf-293154bbefbc&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6e0a9b9cbe21018f536ffe9a9268a305&sv=3)

some3c.com

7.How do I switch between automatic and manual scanning?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-7.how-do-i-switch-between-automatic-and-manual-scanning)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click System Settings. Find the Network Scan Mode option in the Settings pop-up window. Click on Automatic Scan or Manual Scan with your mouse. After the change, you need to restart Panda for it to take effect.

Mode Description:

● Automatic scanning: When casting in OTG mode or WIFI mode, if you restart Panda, it will automatically cast in OTG, WIFI mode

● Manual scanning: When casting in OTG mode or WIFI mode, if you restart Panda, you need to manually scan the IP to cast the screen

![box phone farm kits some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FUXpJIamS372pXflXfN5m%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De9365da0-9c9e-436b-b081-48aec328fdd6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=69b08a753b4088f9e5b1e895495b1e28&sv=3)

some3c.com

8\. What if I can't input?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-8.-what-if-i-cant-input)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Check whether the \[Cast Screen Keyboard\] is installed and enabled normally. The operation demonstration is shown in the figure
    
2.  If it is not installed, click \[Install Cast Screen Keyboard\] first
    
3.  After installation, click Switch to \[Cast Screen Keyboard\] to type, paste and copy normally.
    

![box phone farm kits some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FGCCAI2H1K5WkeJxcyYXO%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcbe66664-8831-4831-8da4-73969bd2c24a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=cce08a416a073208e12bdbbdea5d2f68&sv=3)

some3c.com

9.How to keep the screen of the mobile phone always on?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-9.how-to-keep-the-screen-of-the-mobile-phone-always-on)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Move the mouse to the system settings and left-click, find the phone screen status option and click the Keep on button. After selecting, restart the client to take effect

![Build box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FJYTvSFkcBgDoounVio4f%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1c100dcd-ee96-49ba-9857-cbb5573a4319&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fd13f2a94d7d08ad3e2dd8d246c65fd9&sv=3)

some3c.com

10.How to turn off the mirrored device?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-10.how-to-turn-off-the-mirrored-device)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Click on a device and turn off the Mirror option for that device from the list.

![Build box phone farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FjxlraC19EzQlvZ3Ox67o%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D11df71ac-e835-44ce-a82c-4c867d8644b6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c22c701495b2df9116c2d69fd28a2477&sv=3)

some3c.com

11.How to rotate the small screen[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-11.how-to-rotate-the-small-screen)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Individual rotation

Batch rotation

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FpB4LaAUCrKiSNXpONRHO%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db137d01b-5921-4d2d-bded-6b61edb04869&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6a6aa9e851f0877b5e13a9d7a8307565&sv=3)

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FivqDYLeSlqB7NyeF1Dla%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D5f810adc-ab88-4a57-adbc-f78456b7ef82&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=bffe488c18474394cbc8dbf1606e10f6&sv=3)

12.How to adjust the window size, image quality, and frame rate?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-12.how-to-adjust-the-window-size-image-quality-and-frame-rate)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Drag the slider with the mouse to adjust the size, image quality, and frame rate of the small screen.
    
2.  You can also use the shortcut key ctrl+mouse wheel to adjust the size of the small screen
    

![DIY Phone Farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FQt8sZOkT9c1zQ3ydBAdB%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D97c74f0f-1f9b-40f1-8cd4-32107a3ef51e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=448da3d5c8f5a13de0a32fd77afb55bc&sv=3)

some3c.com

Note:

1.  The higher the frame rate of the small screen, the higher the requirements for the computer and network. If you feel a delay when operating the mobile phone screen, please reduce the frame rate of the small screen to the minimum
    
2.  If the image quality of the small screen is blurred, you can enlarge the size of the small screen
    

13.How to record automated script tasks[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-13.how-to-record-automated-script-tasks)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Right - click on the phone window and select "Record Action." View the video guide.

Can execute custom script tasks. Executing tasks means you need to select a JS file or a bat file. The js file requires the installation of the screen projection assistant, but the bat file does not.

14.How to hide the operation sidebar[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-14.how-to-hide-the-operation-sidebar)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Double-click the window, click the dot, and click the Close option.

![DIY Phone Farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FqVGPPQvNjPaJ6Sc8cPEY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D65c7292b-7ff7-4cde-b177-3d549359418f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a0d309ed918f7ad6c3b8a2916c8b9c15&sv=3)

20.How to use delayed synchronization?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-20.how-to-use-delayed-synchronization)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Click on the panel's more functions, then click on delayed synchronization
    
2.  You can set the minimum time to 1 second and the maximum time to 3600 seconds, and select the operation option that needs to be delayed
    
3.  After setting, close the pop-up window, and the operation of the mobile phone will be delayed according to the sequence number.
    

![phone farm Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F3f9Kwuzd94bt2kkv6QCC%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8db8cc19-aabb-42f0-aa13-5252b159ee34&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d09e58578b86f3ee05e3cdfb5289e3b3&sv=3)

21.How to modify phone parameters?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-21.how-to-modify-phone-parameters)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Click on the panel more functions, then click on the phone parameter option
    
2.  The resolution option allows you to enter the phone resolution value, the DPI option allows you to enter the DPI value, and the battery option allows you to enter a value from 1-00
    
3.  After entering the relevant values, click Confirm to modify the phone and it will take effect immediately, but the resolution and DPI displayed in the "Device List" need to restart the client to refresh.
    
4.  The Restore Phone Default button can restore the selected device resolution and DPI to the default value of the phone, ignoring the battery
    

![phone farm Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fkgi2HiqsLKamzk1mr3HG%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0bf929d1-83da-48b9-831b-baad3595941b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=4bfcce1ba56c97a09b914f54273f6c11&sv=3)

22.How to extract APK?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-22.how-to-extract-apk)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Double-click a single window. In the application list on the right control bar, place the mouse on the APP application icon that needs to extract the APK and a pop-up window will pop up. Find the Extract APK button on the pop-up window, click and confirm
    
2.  The APK is being extracted. The larger the APK, the slower the extraction. Please wait patiently
    
3.  After the extraction is successful, click the Open Directory button to see the extracted APK file
    

![phone farm box some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fpdebp8TawnO0Dmi19bdk%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7233c04c-2bc3-43c7-bf9d-852aa74c9f8b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e8cadb9698dc6b49c5f57f5bb75966a3&sv=3)

23.How to turn on or off small screen control via shortcut keys?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-23.how-to-turn-on-or-off-small-screen-control-via-shortcut-keys)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Use the panel switch to turn on or off the small screen control
    
2.  Click the shortcut bar icon and click the shortcut bar description to find the small screen control shortcut key option. When turned on or off by default, press ctrl+s. You can customize the shortcut key
    

![phone farm box some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FE6Oi3VMVB3Jhe7Z8le8w%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0cdfadd9-a5a7-48b7-8be5-001262e4b949&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1ab7f2748f3a176fa3c4dcdf3d377b17&sv=3)

24.Merge adb authorization files[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-24.merge-adb-authorization-files)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.In the "More" pop-up window, find "Merge Authorization Options" and click

![phone farm box Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FVAKzOFAmPnxnaFvoyIdP%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4dca90e3-d9f2-4acc-8447-f5fbdb790ff7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=09110a2f08903d042e8f1663c7ef32d7&sv=3)

2.In the first step, select the new authorization files "adbkey" and "adbkey.pub", click OK, and **do not select the two authorization files under "C:\\Users\\Administrator\\.android".**

![phone farm box Accessories some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F9yVcvZOUx39Ut2xrSye6%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddb5bffeb-469f-4100-bc35-75c467500dba&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7a1b6f4082403dcbeabde4c68608f8e3&sv=3)

3.Then connect the unauthorized chassis to the computer. Panda will help you merge the authorization files. After the merger is completed, it will automatically go to the third step.

4.The fourth step requires you to manually click the authorization box of the mobile phone window in Panda Screen Mirror, click "Allow" in all mobile phone windows, and then click the "I have authorized all" button. program will complete the final merge, just click Finish.

![phone farm chassis some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FprQDNgT9RbFkt9IQ31q2%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db2ddea39-0df6-45c6-8b3e-391b0de1cdc9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1c1c632ffec4a814d08fbd703ca523f3&sv=3)

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FB2AFsGJRgnEtSEMtvjIs%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3e42b466-238e-4e20-a710-ee8f4b566aa0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=498d5c29726528e9cbd2cd35800b5f69&sv=3)

**We will back up the previous authorization files for you to the backup folder under the Panda installation directory.**

26.How do I use the 【Mobile Sharing】 function?[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/panda-setting-and-use#id-26.how-do-i-use-the-mobile-sharing-function)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**1.Function Introduction** When you need to temporarily transfer the use of your locally connected Android device to someone else, you can initiate device sharing through this feature. The system will establish a connection by generating a unique "sharing code," and the recipient can take full control of the device after entering the code. To ensure device security and business compliance, the sharing party has the highest management privileges and can check the status or forcibly revoke control of the device at any time through "Manage Sharing." **⚠️Important Note:** Login restrictions:Both the sharer and the receiverYou must log in to your accountThis function can only be used when logged in; the relevant entry will be unavailable if you are not logged in. Mutual exclusion logic:Once the device is in "sharing" mode, the sharing party should avoid operating the phone locally to prevent command conflicts with the recipient. Control will only automatically revert to the sharing party when the sharing party actively stops sharing or the sharing expires. **2.Which version supports device sharing?** You can initiate sharing, acquire devices, and manage services independently via the PC client. Supported client versions: Panda Android Screen Mirroring v9.12.213 and above **3.Step 1: Initiate device sharing (sharing party)** Whether you wish to lend out a single device or multiple devices, you can find the sharing option through the following methods: **3.1 Access via right-click menu** Applicable scenarios:When one or more connected local mobile phones are selected on the main interface. Operating instructions:mouseRight-clickSelect the device card (or right-click after selecting multiple devices), and choose from the context menu that appears."Shared devices".

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FaniIdcZvIPHlgL0ZGd0Q%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260826185955.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D55175676-aef1-40ce-8583-b743260e3aec&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=717d511796abf03234c97268beca0131&sv=3)

**3.2 Setting the expiration date and generating a sharing code** Operating instructions:Clicking will bring up a time selection drawer; please select "Share".Deadline/Time. Tag Management:After confirming the time, a 9-digit sharing code will be generated in a pop-up window. You can directly copy the sharing code; the system will automatically generate a default tag, or you can edit the tag for better recognition. Click "OK" to close the pop-up window.

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FbEHzPgMzFEdTeOr0Rixf%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260826190155.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D26587125-72fd-4d4c-bebd-62040561cf71&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=18f0a15281de7303b7a28fd246e35837&sv=3)

**3.3 Changes in Machine Status** Results feedback:After successful sharing, the device card interface will change: a green indicator will appear in the upper left corner.Share iconThe middle area is displayedShare codeandExpiry dateThe bottom status bar displays "Screen casting is normal".

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FkJqzfK8rxpYrFTKR3qaf%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260826190244.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3a21d28d-3311-4e50-aeaf-d1082dee9b63&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=93f7183c27550225962faec1d66fe4c2&sv=3)

**4.Step 2: Obtaining and using the equipment (the acquiring party)** 4.1 Accessing the Entry Point Operating instructions:After logging into the client, the recipient clicks on the top menu bar.More features-> Select"Get Share".

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F1jvmWUYC88CoGeeBRXvr%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da5da3914-1c24-4f26-8750-40713bd811f6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=aea6f2d979d5246bde54dd4eabaa9581&sv=3)

**4.2 Enter the sharing code** Operating instructions:In the pop-up "Get Sharing Device" window, enter the 9-digit sharing code provided by the sharing provider, and click on the right.“Identification”Button.

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fy4lGVBs2rb3NRxKEh2Ou%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd09f4746-014c-4015-a04c-5a73a18a402f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9f015088ec55afd420c27e06a5d47517&sv=3)

**4.3 Confirm Acquisition** Operating instructions:After successful system recognition, a device information preview will be loaded. Click"Confirm Acquisition"Click the button, and the system will display "Retrieval successful". **4.4 Getting Started** Results feedback:After a short wait, the sharing device will appear in the device list. The card will display the word "Share" and the specific expiration time. Double-click to connect and control it.

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fg5BeYl6UiyhD9GZQ369U%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfc02bc5f-af76-4a1a-8298-fdc0c62bf213&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6f1054aebd477b3a0d8062156fd53d8d&sv=3)

**5.Step 3: Managing and Stopping Sharing (by the Sharing Party)** **5.1 Check sharing status** Operating instructions:The sharer clicks the top menu bar.More features-> Select"Management Sharing". Interface description:On the management page, you can quickly filter devices and view the current sharing status using the three tabs: "All," "Sharing," and "Expired."

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F0BSSAZTnWuevXZIyL86o%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260826191750.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D55c2bf35-57b3-4de8-80c1-008f8b5616ee&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0b1d10fe2de59e4a4e1efe14af4ac2c6&sv=3)

**5.2 Stop sharing (revoke permissions)** Applicable scenarios:When it is necessary to terminate the equipment loan early, or when an anomaly is discovered and control needs to be regained immediately. Operating instructions: In the "Manage Shares" list or device list, find the device that is being shared. Click on the device cardStop sharingButton (or right-click menu to select Stop).

In the pop-up confirmation box, select the stop range (single unit or batch), and click.Confirm to stop sharing.

![Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display & control Android on PC | some 3C ](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FUU2eOsVTjM5ryDGBnUHW%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260826190430.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deb748b5f-81c5-4504-8432-14e699fa8ffa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=db7c8027b1e5bb063603c9623c3d7a2a&sv=3)

# Panda Forward | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/panda-forward.md)
.

Features[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-classic/panda-forward#features)

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Panda Forward is a P2P intranet penetration tool based on WebRTC technology, allowing you to easily achieve TCP port forwarding across local area networks. Whether you want to remotely access your home computer or connect to your home phone from the office, Panda Forward helps you establish secure and fast point-to-point connections.

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FsXt6ucHGsTaqhMYjMhXv%252FQQ%25E6%2588%25AA%25E5%259B%25BE20260423060701.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D665b9454-703b-4ad6-b33c-290ea4359fed&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7b2f2556ca7f956e616673f3552db73c&sv=3)

.

1\. System requirements[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/imouse-xp-new-version#id-1.-system-requirements)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   System: Windows 10 or later
    
*   RAM: 8GB or higher
    
*   CPU: Dual-core or higher, with hardware acceleration preferred
    
*   Display resolution: 1920×1080 or higher recommended
    

2.Installation preparation[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/imouse-xp-new-version#id-2.installation-preparation)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Before downloading the installer, it is recommended to add the download directory to the Windows security exclusion list in advance

3.Language switching[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/imouse-xp-new-version#id-3.language-switching)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.Click "系统设置" and change the language in the "语言" option.

![imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FqMGx52gMW2UoXEy7vXTB%252F%25E8%25AF%25AD%25E8%25A8%2580.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8d074219-f81e-46ee-a06e-bb7cdc740109&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=28c1db6b41f42b447b533f878fd4dcde&sv=3)

4.Deactivating and starting kernels[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/imouse-xp-new-version#id-4.deactivating-and-starting-kernels)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

iMouse is divided into two parts: \[Core\] and \[Console\] \[Console\] is an interface that provides users with computer and mobile phone interaction. After exiting the console, the kernel still keeps running. \[Kernel\] is the core part of the iMouse that communicates with the hardware and the phone. It is a Windows service running without an interface.

If the console is abnormal, you can usually try restarting the kernel. If you need to change the version or uninstall the software, please disable and uninstall the kernel first.

![imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252Fc5Z4UNF2fSHrDrqPEiIl%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9b63e5f1-1238-4fa8-9568-e7bea922d7d1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e84145f1351b81d2c330e8eff40bf71d&sv=3)

Or you can find the corresponding file in the installation folder directory

# iPhone Farm Settings | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings.md)
.

**Supports all models of iPhone 6s and above IOS system 13.4 and above**

For Usage Notes[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#for-usage-notes)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Note the correct orientation of the default USB small board. If you insert the small board incorrectly and cause a short - circuit burnout, you will bear the risk yourself.**

![iphone box  phone  farm  some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FNvNSBuXiEquq04HRG0Vz%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De0f74629-cc42-4549-a951-53c1d528bf4d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7e90a5157c46b053290fc4cb0ae8e344&sv=3)

some3c.com

**Do not allow any cable ends or metal parts to come into contact with the yellow PCBA area, as this may cause a short circuit in the components and damage to the chassis.**

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FZK2zonaZ1QPcMRQRM349%252FIMG_9151.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0c76e988-f3c3-4d0d-bee5-9994b8dcf232&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f1e4aeb8a072009d67cf564e20e56e24&sv=3)

1.Connect iPhone to control panel[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-1.connect-iphone-to-control-panel)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Connect the USB C cable to the "电脑" port on the right side of the control board.

![Controlling iPhone via computer some3c.com](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FarBde0rJJsIwqYMDf2Lj%252F%25E5%259B%25BE%25E7%2589%25873.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7faea76a-463b-45f5-b5b5-6ae719b64888&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e91de9f65702fee8bd7db29cf55f3635&sv=3)

some3c.com

cart-shopping-fast

[**Click to purchase the control board**](https://some3c.com/products/iphone-jailbreak-free-central-control-ios-automation-script-otg-game-studio-hardware-synchronizer-screencast-control-board)

2.iPhone Setting[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-2.iphone-setting)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.Enable Assistivetouch[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-1.enable-assistivetouch)

**Settings-Accessibility-Touch-Assistivetouch(ON)-(AlwaysShowMenu-close Tracking Sensitivity -Max)**

**Layout of the control center is based on the hierarchical layout of the corresponding model in the figure below.**

The core is that there must be "two rows of icons" at the bottom. It doesn't matter what function icons they are. Settings-->Control Center (+ and - will do), (note that this setting affects automatic screencasting)

Then select Mirror from the Control Center.

The phone must be on the same network as the computer. After mirroring, the phone screen will appear on the computer.

(If it does not appear, then please check the network.)

3.Computer software settings[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-3.computer-software-settings)

### 1.PC and iphone must be same network[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-1.pc-and-iphone-must-be-same-network)

Make sure that the mobile phone and computer are on the same network segment. If the system is brand new, please install the VC runtime library.exe first

### 2.Installation environment[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-2.installation-environment)

If the computer is equipped with other anti-virus software such as 360 or Windows Security. If so, be sure to trust (iMouse Console Professional Edition) or exit. Otherwise the software will not work properly. Recommended to uninstall

4.Internet settings via SIM Card[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings#id-4.internet-settings-via-sim-card)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To access the internet using cellular data, please refer to the following steps.

1.  Open Settings -> Wi-Fi -> Connected Wi-Fi -> Edit
    
2.  IPv4 address -> Click Edit and select Manual Mode
    
3.  Enter the IP address and subnet mask. Do not enter the router/gateway field! Do not enter the router/gateway field!
    
4.  Save and the changes will take effect immediately without affecting screen mirroring. If using a wired connection, the entry point is Settings -> Ethernet.

# XP API Documentation | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation.md)
.

This interface can be accessed and interacted using http and websocket `websocket` and `http` port numbers are "`9911`"

http request[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation#http-qing-qiu-fang-shi)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Interface address** `iP address:9911/api` Example:`http://127.0.0.1:9911/api`
    
*   **Request method** `get` , `post submitjson`,`post form`
    

Websocket request[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation#websocket-qing-qiu-fang-shi)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Using websocket to request an interface can get a faster response, but it is more complicated than http request. All request results are in the callback function. Because it is an asynchronous method, if you want to wait for the request result before proceeding to the next step, you need to perform asynchronous to synchronous conversion. Each interface will have a msgid field, which will be returned as is after the server receives it, and is used for asynchronous to synchronous conversion.5

*   **Interface address** `ws://server address:9911/api` example`:ws://127.0.0.1:9911/api`
    
*   **Request content format** `json`
    

All interfaces will return success or failure after calling. `status`200 represents success, and other values ​​represent failure. The failure information will be in`message` the result of the call , which represents the actual execution result.`datadatacode`

code

id does not exist

3

4

# Python library | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library.md)
.

1.Quick[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#kuai-su-kai-shi)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Before use, use pip to install the imouse-py package
    
*   `pip install imouse-py`
    

2.Use the API class basic interface[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#shi-yong-api-lei-ji-chu-jie-kou-diao-yong)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    import imouse
    from imouse.types import MouseSwipeParams
    
    # Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
    api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the api instance. All interfaces provided by iMouse are called in the api instance.
    
    # Perform mouse operations through the methods in the API class
    api.mouse_click("FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8", "", 100, 100)  # Left click screen coordinates (100, 100)
    api.mouse_swipe("FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8", params=MouseSwipeParams(  # Swipe up the screen from the bottom 10% to 90%
        direction='up',  # 
        len=0.9
    ))
    

3.Easier calling through helper class (recommended)[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#id-3.easier-calling-through-helper-class-recommended)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### 1.API provided by Console[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#id-1.api-provided-by-console)

Provides access to device management and global operations:

*   **Device**: Device Management
    
*   **AirPlay**: Screen projection connection and configuration
    
*   **USB**: imouse Hardware Management
    
*   **Group**: Group management
    
*   **ImConfig**: iMouse Global configuration management
    
*   **User**: iMouse Account Management
    

### 2.API provided by device[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#id-2.api-provided-by-device)

Provides control over individual devices:

*   **Image**: Device image operations, such as screenshots, image search, text recognition, etc.
    
*   **KeyBoard**: Keyboard operation
    
*   **Mouse**: Mouse Operation
    
*   **Shortcut**: Shortcut command operation
    

### 3.iMouse Event Handling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#imouse-shi-jian-chu-li)

### 4.Configuring iMouse Log Output[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/python-library#id-4.configuring-imouse-log-output)

    import imouse
    
    # Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
    api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the API instance
    
    helper = imouse.helper(api)  # Get the helper class instance
    
    console = helper.console  # Get a console instance
    ret = console.device.list_by_id()  # Get a list of all devices
    print(ret)
    # Cast screen to designated device
    ret = console.airplay.connect('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8,FD:9E:10:3A:FE:E0')
    if ret:
        print('成功')
    else:
        print(f'失败:{console.error_msg}')
    
    # 断开指定设备投屏
    ret = console.airplay.disconnect('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8,FD:9E:10:3A:FE:E0')
    if ret:
        print('成功')
    else:
        print(f'失败:{console.error_msg}')
    
    # 投屏所有离线设备
    ret = console.airplay.connect_all()
    if ret:
        print('成功')
    else:
        print(f'失败:{console.error_msg}')

Copy

    import imouse
    from imouse.utils import file_to_base64
    from imouse.types import MouseSwipeParams
    
    # Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
    api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the API instance
    
    helper = imouse.helper(api)  # Get the helper class instance
    
    device = helper.device('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8')  # Get the device instance by device ID
    
    # You can also get a list of all instances through the devices method
    # device_list = helper.devices()
    # device = device_list[0]
    
    # screenshot
    ret = device.image.screenshot()
    if ret:
        print('截图成功')
        with open('test.bmp', "wb") as f:
            f.write(ret)
    else:
        print(f'截图失败:{device.error_msg}')
    
    # Find images through opencv
    img_str = file_to_base64("test1.bmp")
    ret = device.image.find_image_cv([img_str])
    if len(ret) > 0:
        print(f"找图成功[{ret[0].centre[0]},{ret[0].centre[1]}]")
    else:
        print(f'找图失败:{device.error_msg}')
    
    # 向上滑动屏幕
    ret = device.mouse.swipe(MouseSwipeParams(direction='up', len=0.9))  # Swipe from the bottom 10 percent of the screen to 90 percent of the screen
    if ret:
        print('滑动成功')
    else:
        print(f'滑动失败:{device.error_msg}')
    

Copy

    from typing import List
    import imouse
    from imouse.api import event
    from imouse.models import DeviceInfo, UsbInfo, UserData, ImServerConfigData
    
    
    @event.on("im_connect")
    def im_connect(ver: str):
        print(f"[Event] Connect to kernel successfully: {ver}")
    
    
    @event.on("im_disconnect")
    def im_disconnect():
        print(f"[Event] Disconnected from kernel")
    
    
    @event.on("dev_connect")
    def dev_connect(device_info: DeviceInfo):
        print("[Event] A device is connected" + device_info.device_id)
    
    
    @event.on("dev_disconnect")
    def dev_disconnect(device_info: DeviceInfo):
        print("[Event] A device was disconnected->" + device_info.device_id)
    
    
    @event.on("dev_rotate")
    def dev_rotate(device_info: DeviceInfo):
        print("[Event] The device rotates->" + device_info.device_id)
    
    
    @event.on("dev_change")
    def dev_change(device_info: DeviceInfo):
        print("[Event] Equipment changed->" + device_info.device_id)
    
    
    @event.on("dev_delete")
    def dev_delete(deviceid_list: List[str]):
        print(f"[Event] Device deleted->{deviceid_list}")
    
    
    @event.on("group_change")
    def group_change(gid: str, name: str):
        print(f"[Event] There is a group change->{gid},{name}")
    
    
    @event.on("group_change")
    def group_change(gid: str, name: str):
        print(f"[Event] There is a group change->{gid},{name}")
    
    
    @event.on("group_delete")
    def group_delete(gid_list: List[str]):
        print(f"[Event] Group deletion->{gid_list}")
    
    
    @event.on("usb_change")
    def usb_change(usb_info: UsbInfo):
        print(f"[Event] USB device changed->{usb_info}")
    
    
    @event.on("airplay_connect_log")
    def usb_change(message: str):
        print(f"[Event] Automatic screen projection log->{message}")
    
    
    @event.on("user_info")
    def user_info(data: UserData):
        print(f"[Event] User information status->{data}")
    
    
    @event.on("im_log")
    def im_log(message: str):
        print(f"[Event]iMouse event->{message}")
    
    
    @event.on("error_push")
    def error_push(message: str, call_fun: str):
        print(f"[Event] iMouse Error Log->{message},{call_fun}")
    
    
    @event.on("im_config_change")
    def im_config_change(config: ImServerConfigData):
        print(f"[Event] iMouse kernel configuration changed->{config}")
    
    
    @event.on("logout")
    def logout():
        print(f"[Event] iMouse account exit-")
    
    
    @event.on("dev_sort_change")
    def dev_sort_change(sort_index: int, sort_value: int):
        print(f"[Event] iMouse device list sorting changed->{sort_index},{sort_value}")
    
    
    api = imouse.api(host='192.168.9.9')

    import logging
    from imouse.utils import logger
    
    logger.configure(
        is_debug=True,  # Whether to enable debug logging (info/debug will not be output when False)
        name='imouse',  # Log name (affects logger name and log file name)
        log_dir='logs',  # Log directory (default logs folder)
        log_level=logging.DEBUG,  # Log level
        log_show_thread_id=False,  # Whether to display thread ID
        log_show_file_and_line=False  #Whether to display file and line numbers
    )

# Callback | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/callback.md)
.

1.Callback Description[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/callback#id-1.callback-description)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Callback[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/callback#hui-tiao-shuo-ming)

Although callbacks and asynchronous requests appear in the ws callback, the callback mentioned here is the message that the kernel actively broadcasts to all connected clients.

For example, if a connected client deletes a device, sets device operations, or takes a device offline, the kernel will automatically push messages to all connected clients.

### Callback[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/callback#hui-tiao-ge-shi)

Copy

    {
      "fun": "Function number",
      "status": Must be 200
      "message": "",
      "msgid": 0,
      "data": {}Actual data
    }

illustrate

The error message is in message, and call\_fun is what function is called.

# Picture Text Recognition

## 1.Take a screenshot

In special cases, you can use the shortcut (shortcut version 1.3 or higher) /shortcut/other/screenshot

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter  | type          | illustrate                      | Remark                                            |
| ---------- | ------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| fun        | String        | /pic/screenshot                 | Request function name                             |
| id         | String        | Device ID                       | Multiple is not allowed                           |
| binary     | Boolean       | If true, returns binary data.   | By default, json is used for return               |
| jpg        | Boolean       | If true, returns the jpg format | Default bmp format                                |
| rect       | Integer Array | Leave full screen blank         | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y                     |
| save\\\_path | String        | Save to local path              | If left blank, return using memory stream or json |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/screenshot?id=5C:F7:E6:CE:65:22&save\_path=D%3A%5CiMouseXP%5CScreenshot%5C'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \\
  --form fun=/pic/screenshot \\
  --form id=5C:F7:E6:CE:65:22
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
    "fun": "/pic/screenshot",
    "data": {
        "id":"5C:F7:E6:CE:65:22",
        "binary":true
    }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

When the binary parameter is true, this json will not be returned, and the binary image data will be returned directly

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "6C:72:E7:EA:CD:95",
    "jpg": false,
    "binary": false,
    "rect": "",
    "image": "D:\\\\iMouseXP\\\\Screenshot\\\\1128935437.bmp",
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/screenshot"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data field description\*\*

| Fields | illustrate | Remark                                                                                    |
| ------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| jpg    | -          | -                                                                                         |
| binary | -          | -                                                                                         |
| rect   | -          | -                                                                                         |
| image  | Image data | If save\\\_path is used, the image path is returned, otherwise the base64 string image data |

## 2.General image search

In special cases, you can use the shortcut (shortcut version 1.3 or higher) /shortcut/other/find-image

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

This image search mode is exactly the same as DM

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter    | type                   | illustrate          | Remark                                                                                          |
| ------------ | ---------------------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| fun          | String                 | /pic/find-image     | Request function name                                                                           |
| id           | String                 | Device ID           | Multiple is not allowed                                                                         |
| target\\\_img  | String                 | Big picture of goal | Generally, you don't need to fill it in. Our internal screen image will be used by default.     |
| delta\\\_color | String                 | Color Cast          | Reference DM                                                                                    |
| all          | Boolean                | Have you found all? | false will only return one of the images in the list, true will find all the images in the list |
| direction    | String                 | Find directions     | Reference DM                                                                                    |
| img\\\_list    | String Array           | Thumbnail           | Base64 string image list or local path list                                                     |
| rect         | Integer Array          | Find Region         | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y                                                                   |
| similarity   | Floating point numbers | Similarity          | Greater than 0 and less than 1                                                                  |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/find-image?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29&similarity=0.8&img\_list=%5B%22D%3A%5C%5CiMouseXP%5C%5CScreenshot%5C%5C1.bmp%22%5D'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header "content-type: multipart/form-data" \\
  --form "fun=/pic/find-image" \\
  --form "id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29" \\
  --form "similarity=0.8" \\
  --form "img\_list=\[\\"D:\\\\\\iMouseXP\\\\\\Screenshot\\\\\\1.bmp\\"\]"
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/find-image",
     "data": {
          "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
          "img\_list": \["D:\\\\iMouseXP\\\\Screenshot\\\\1.bmp"\],
          "similarity": 0.85
     }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
    "list": \[\
      {\
        "index": 0,\
        "centre": \[\
          457,\
          1231\
        \],\
        "rect": \[\
          425,\
          1206,\
          489,\
          1257\
        \],\
        "code": 0\
      }\
    \],
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/find-image"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data field description\*\*

| Fields | illustrate                              | Remark                                      |
| ------ | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| index  | index                                   | The first picture is found, starting from 0 |
| centre | Find the center point of the image      | x and y                                     |
| rect   | Find the rectangular region of an image | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y               |

## 3.OpenCV Image Search

In special cases, you can use the shortcut (shortcut version 1.3 or higher): /shortcut/other/find-image-cv

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter   | type                   | illustrate                         | Remark                                                                                          |
| ----------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| fun         | String                 | /pic/find-image-cv                 | Request function name                                                                           |
| id          | String                 | Device ID                          | Multiple is not allowed                                                                         |
| target\\\_img | String                 | Big picture of goal                | Generally, you don't need to fill it in. Our internal screen image will be used by default.     |
| same        | Boolean                | Do you want to find the same image | If true, all the same images in the image will be found                                         |
| all         | Boolean                | Have you found all                 | false will only return one of the images in the list, true will find all the images in the list |
| img\\\_list   | String Array           | Thumbnail                          | Base64 string image list or local path list                                                     |
| rect        | Integer Array          | Find Region                        | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y                                                                   |
| similarity  | Floating point numbers | Similarity                         | Greater than 0 and less than 1                                                                  |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/find-image-cv?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29&similarity=0.8&img\_list=%5B%22D%3A%5C%5CiMouseXP%5C%5CScreenshot%5C%5C1.bmp%22%5D'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header "content-type: multipart/form-data" \\
  --form "fun=/pic/find-image-cv" \\
  --form "id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29" \\
  --form "similarity=0.8" \\
  --form "img\_list=\[\\"D:\\\\\\iMouseXP\\\\\\Screenshot\\\\\\1.bmp\\"\]"
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/find-image-cv",
     "data": {
          "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
          "img\_list": \["D:\\\\iMouseXP\\\\Screenshot\\\\1.bmp"\],
          "similarity": 0.85
     }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
    "list": \[\
      {\
        "index": 0,\
        "centre": \[\
          457,\
          1231\
        \],\
        "rect": \[\
          425,\
          1206,\
          489,\
          1257\
        \],\
        "code": 0\
      }\
    \],
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/find-image-cv"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data Data field description\*\*\\
  list returns a list of all found text

| Fields     | illustrate                              | Remark                                      |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| index      | index                                   | The first picture is found, starting from 0 |
| centre     | Find the center point of the image      | x and y                                     |
| rect       | Find the rectangular region of an image | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y               |
| similarity | Similarity                              |                                             |

## 4.OCR text recognition

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

In special cases, shortcuts can be used (shortcut version 1.3 or higher required): /shortcut/other/ocr /shortcut/other/ocr-ex\\
There's another feature called /pic/ocr-ex, an enhanced OCR version with more accurate recognition, but it uses more resources.

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter   | type          | illustrate          | Remark                                                                                      |
| ----------- | ------------- | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| fun         | String        | /pic/ocr            | Request function name                                                                       |
| id          | String        | Device ID           | Multiple is not allowed                                                                     |
| target\\\_img | String        | Big picture of goal | Generally, you don't need to fill it in. Our internal screen image will be used by default. |
| rect        | Integer Array | Find Region         | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y                                                               |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/ocr?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header "content-type: multipart/form-data" \\
  --form "fun=/pic/ocr" \\
  --form "id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29"
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/ocr",
     "data": {
          "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29"
     }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
    "list": \[\
      {\
        "text": "Screen mirror output",\
        "centre": \[\
          374,\
          475\
        \],\
        "rect": \[\
          270,\
          457,\
          478,\
          494\
        \],\
        "similarity": 0.82\
      }\
    \],
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/ocr"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data Data field description\*\*\\
  list returns a list of all found text

| Fields     | illustrate                   | Remark                        |
| ---------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| text       | Recognized text              |                               |
| centre     | The center point of the text | x and y                       |
| rect       | Rectangular area of ​​text   | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y |
| similarity | Similarity                   |                               |

## 5.Find text

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

In special cases, shortcuts can be used (shortcut version 1.3 or higher required): /shortcut/other/find-text /shortcut/other/find-text-ex

There is also a function called /pic/find-text-ex that uses an enhanced version of OCR that is more accurate but takes up more resources.

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter   | type                   | illustrate                    | Remark                                                                                      |
| ----------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| fun         | String                 | /pic/find-text                | Request function name                                                                       |
| id          | String                 | Device ID                     | Multiple is not allowed                                                                     |
| target\\\_img | String                 | Big picture of goal           | Generally, you don't need to fill it in. Our internal screen image will be used by default. |
| rect        | Integer Array          | Find Region                   | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y                                                               |
| similarity  | Floating point numbers | Similarity                    | It is best to use OCR to recognize it once to see how similar it is.                        |
| contain     | Boolean                | Contains                      |                                                                                             |
| text        | String Array           | The text array to be searched |                                                                                             |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/find-text?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29&contain=true&similarity=0.8&text=%5B%22D2B6%22%2C%22DCA1%22%5D'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header "content-type: multipart/form-data" \\
  --form "fun=/pic/find-text" \\
  --form 'text=\["aaa","bbb"\]' \\
  --form "similarity=0.8" \\
  --form "id=FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8"
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/find-text",
     "data": {
          "id": "FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8",
          "text":\["时钟","邮件"\],
          "similarity":0.8
     }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
    "list": \[\
      {\
        "text": "iMouseXP-D2B6",\
        "centre": \[\
          283,\
          581\
        \],\
        "rect": \[\
          153,\
          565,\
          413,\
          598\
        \],\
        "similarity": 0.98\
      },\
      {\
        "text": "iMouseXP-DCA1",\
        "centre": \[\
          281,\
          685\
        \],\
        "rect": \[\
          153,\
          671,\
          409,\
          700\
        \],\
        "similarity": 0.91\
      }\
    \],
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/find-text"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data Data field description\*\*\\
  list returns a list of all found text

| Fields     | illustrate                   | Remark                        |
| ---------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| text       | Recognized text              |                               |
| centre     | The center point of the text | x and y                       |
| rect       | Rectangular area of ​​text   | Left x, Up y, Right x, Down y |
| similarity | Similarity                   |                               |

## 6.Find Multi Color

In special cases, you can use the shortcut (shortcut version 1.3 or higher) /shortcut/other/find-multi-color

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter   | type              | illustrate                          | Remark                                                                                          |
| ----------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| fun         | String            | /pic/find-multi-color               | Request function name                                                                           |
| id          | String            | Device ID                           | Multiple is not allowed                                                                         |
| target\\\_img | String            | Big picture of goal                 | Generally, you don't need to fill it in. Our internal screen image will be used by default.     |
| same        | Boolean           | Do you want to find the same image? | If true, all the same color tables in the image will be found                                   |
| all         | Boolean           | Have you found all?                 | false will return only one of the colors in the list, true will find all the colors in the list |
| list        | json object array | -                                   | -                                                                                               |

Parameter description of json object array in list:

| parameter    | type                   | illustrate                     | Remark                                                               |
| ------------ | ---------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| first\\\_color | String                 | The color table being searched | -                                                                    |
| rect         | Integer Array          | Find Region                    | Left x, top y, right x, bottom y are empty and search in full screen |
| similarity   | Floating point numbers | Similarity                     | -                                                                    |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
--url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/pic/find-multi-color?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29&list=%5B%7B%22first\_color%22%3A%20%22284%7C1224%7C2AB9F3-000000%2C310%7C1235%7C29ADF1-000000%2C281%7C1225%7C29B8F2-000000%2C288%7C1236%7CEB2A31-000000%2C286%7C1258%7C2693EC-000000%2C262%7C1253%7C2699EE-000000%2C265%7C1228%7C2AB5F2-000000%2C305%7C1232%7CC25B78-000000%2C336%7C1253%7C2997EE-000000%2C301%7C1258%7C2693EC-000000%2C281%7C1245%7CFFFFFC-000000%22%2C%22similarity%22%3A%200.85%7D%5D'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
--url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
--header "Content-Type: multipart/form-data" \\
--form "fun=/pic/find-multi-color" \\
--form "id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29" \\
--form "list=\[{\\"first\_color\\":\\"284|1224|2AB9F3-000000,310|1235|29ADF1-000000,281|1225|29B8F2-000000,288|1236|EB2A31-000000,286|1258|2693EC-000000,262|1253|2699EE-000000,265|1228|2AB5F2-000000,305|1232|C25B78-000000,336|1253|2997EE-000000,301|1258|2693EC-000000,281|1245|FFFFFC-000000\\",\\"similarity\\":0.85}\]"
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/find-multi-color",
     "data": {
          "id": "60:9A:C1:9A:22:A1",
          "list": \[\
               {\
                    "first\_color": "284|1224|2AB9F3-000000,310|1235|29ADF1-000000,281|1225|29B8F2-000000,288|1236|EB2A31-000000,286|1258|2693EC-000000,262|1253|2699EE-000000,265|1228|2AB5F2-000000,305|1232|C25B78-000000,336|1253|2997EE-000000,301|1258|2693EC-000000,281|1245|FFFFFC-000000",\
                    "similarity": 0.85\
               }\
          \],
          "same": false,
          "all": false
     }
}'
\`\`\`

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
     "fun": "/pic/find-multi-color",
     "data": {
          "id": "6C:8D:C1:67:04:29",
          "list": \[\
               {\
                    "rect": \[\
                         0,\
                         0,\
                         0,\
                         0\
                    \],\
                    "first\_color": "284|1224|2AB9F3-000000,310|1235|29ADF1-000000,281|1225|29B8F2-000000,288|1236|EB2A31-000000,286|1258|2693EC-000000,262|1253|2699EE-000000,265|1228|2AB5F2-000000,305|1232|C25B78-000000,336|1253|2997EE-000000,301|1258|2693EC-000000,281|1245|FFFFFC-000000",\
                    "similarity": 0.85\
               }\
          \],
          "same": false,
          "all": false
     }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "60:9A:C1:9A:22:A1",
    "list": \[\
      {\
        "index": 0,\
        "centre": \[\
          286,\
          1222\
        \]\
      }\
    \],
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/pic/find-multi-color"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data Data field description\*\*\\
  list returns a list of all found text

| Fields | illustrate                         | Remark                                                         |
| ------ | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| index  | index                              | The first color search configuration is found, starting from 0 |
| centre | Find the center point of the image | x and y                                                        |

# Keyboard and Mouse | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse.md)
.

1.Mouse clicks[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse#id-1.mouse-clicks)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse#diao-yong-shuo-ming)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/mouse/click

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

Separate multiple devices with commas

button

String

1 left button, 2 right button, 3 middle button, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8 button

If it is empty, the default left key is supported, and up to 8 keys are supported

x

Integer

x-coordinate

\-

y

Integer

y coordinate

\-

time

Integer

The delay between pressing and popping up, if not filled in, it will be handled by the internal

millisecond

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/mouse/click?id=5C:F7:E6:CE:65:22&x=100&y=100'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \
      --form fun=/mouse/click  \
      --form id=5C:F7:E6:CE:65:22 \
      --form x=100 \
      --form y=100 

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**
    

### Response[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse#xiang-ying-jie-guo)

*   **Response data data field description** None
    

2.Mouse slide[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse#id-2.mouse-slide)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/keyboard-and-mouse#diao-yong-shuo-ming-1)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/mouse/swipe

Request function name

id

String

Device unique ID

Separate multiple devices with commas

button

String

1 left button, 2 right button

Empty default left button

direction

Integer

Slide direction up, down, left, right

Corresponding to up, down, left and right respectively

len

Floating point numbers

Slide distance percentage

Assume 0.9 will slide from 10% to 90% of the screen

step\_sleep

Integer

When steping is greater than 1, it takes effect

The interval between multiple slides in milliseconds

stepping

Integer

How many times to slide to the specified position in the loop

\-

brake

Integer

Stop immediately after sliding

Some interfaces may be delayed after sliding

sx

Integer

y coordinate

0 Automatically calculated using the sliding distance percentage

sy

Integer

y coordinate

0 Automatically calculated using the sliding distance percentage

ex

Integer

y coordinate

0 Automatically calculated using the sliding distance percentage

ey

Integer

y coordinate

0 Automatically calculated using the sliding distance percentage

*   **httpGet request example**
    

*   **httpPost request example**

# Unknown
