# Panda Setting & Use

## 1.How to switch the interface to dark mode?

Place the mouse on the control panel icon and left-click to switch the theme of the entire Panda Android screen projection interface.

<figure><img src="/files/FtvQgP7rZKfREQw0x5Nh" alt="box phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 2.How do I switch theme colors?

Click System Settings, find the theme color option in the settings pop-up window, and click the color block with your mouse to select the theme color.

<figure><img src="/files/KL0SZ3wsJdUnGs1RJ5K7" alt="box phone farm Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 3.How to fold and secure the control panel?

Put the mouse on the control panel and left-click to collapse the panel. If you want to fix the panel, move the mouse to the rightmost side of the software and the control panel will appear. At this time, click with the mouse to fix the control panel.

<figure><img src="/files/Ypcy0CQVWJCrVjEFdZ9K" alt="box phone farm Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 4.How do I switch the control panel to the left?

Click System Settings. Find the Control Panel Position option in the Settings pop-up window and click Left or Right. The control panel will switch left and right.

<figure><img src="/files/HiAjYJHMB5R5gDsP1cgN" alt="box phone farm Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 5.How to control single and batch?

<table><thead><tr><th width="388">Individual control</th><th>Batch control</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><p>Click the magnifying glass of the small window, or double-click a separate window to control it separately.<br></p><p><img src="/files/8iRh9CSgja8usF8YbL6C" alt=""></p></td><td>Click the magnifying glass of the small winUse the mouse to select some or all of them, then double-click or click the magnifying glass in one of the windows to control the batch.<br><img src="/files/WzWXphwd3SBkJNhe0E7R" alt=""></td></tr></tbody></table>

## 6.How to hide the magnifying glass button on small screens

Click System Settings. Find the small screen magnifier option in the settings pop-up window. Click it to show or hide it.

<figure><img src="/files/ZhajJ31hQ7LizlAOhboW" alt="box phone farm kits some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 7.How do I switch between automatic and manual scanning?

Click System Settings. Find the Network Scan Mode option in the Settings pop-up window. Click on Automatic Scan or Manual Scan with your mouse. After the change, you need to restart Panda for it to take effect.

{% hint style="info" %}
Mode Description:&#x20;

● Automatic scanning: When casting in OTG mode or WIFI mode, if you restart Panda, it will automatically cast in OTG, WIFI mode&#x20;

● Manual scanning: When casting in OTG mode or WIFI mode, if you restart Panda, you need to manually scan the IP to cast the screen
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/UyYaJIxAD6WxF1FTzKg9" alt="box phone farm kits some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 8. What if I can't input?

1. Check whether the \\\[Cast Screen Keyboard\] is installed and enabled normally. The operation demonstration is shown in the figure
2. If it is not installed, click \\\[Install Cast Screen Keyboard\] first
3. After installation, click Switch to \\\[Cast Screen Keyboard\] to type, paste and copy normally.

<figure><img src="/files/aTCSNqMPKunXWNfKigHD" alt="box phone farm kits some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 9.How to keep the screen of the mobile phone always on?

Move the mouse to the system settings and left-click, find the phone screen status option and click the Keep on button. After selecting, restart the client to take effect

<figure><img src="/files/FVOfHUN3xQlgH1JTrgHr" alt="Build box phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 10.How to turn off the mirrored device?

Click on a device and turn off the Mirror option for that device from the list.

<figure><img src="/files/NfOEQwEKIz5ww9fTFeIP" alt="Build box phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

## 11.How to rotate the small screen

| Individual rotation                                                 | Batch rotation                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| <img src="/files/fRITaGvDCdgN5O2VAUO3" alt="" data-size="original"> | <img src="/files/UL7wSjQYx2qERBXRVOmO" alt="" data-size="original"> |

## 12.How to adjust the window size, image quality, and frame rate?

1. Drag the slider with the mouse to adjust the size, image quality, and frame rate of the small screen.
2. You can also use the shortcut key ctrl+mouse wheel to adjust the size of the small screen

<figure><img src="/files/5TgwVEjJSrelYuB7InsZ" alt="DIY Phone Farm some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %}

Note:

1. The higher the frame rate of the small screen, the higher the requirements for the computer and network. If you feel a delay when operating the mobile phone screen, please reduce the frame rate of the small screen to the minimum
2. If the image quality of the small screen is blurred, you can enlarge the size of the small screen
   {% endhint %}

## 13.How to record automated script tasks

Right - click on the phone window and select "Record Action." View the video guide.

Can execute custom script tasks. Executing tasks means you need to select a JS file or a bat file. The js file requires the installation of the screen projection assistant, but the bat file does not.

{% embed url="<https://youtu.be/XtnPYmd0D0Q>" %}

## 14.How to hide the operation sidebar

Double-click the window, click the dot, and click the Close option.

<figure><img src="/files/QyWA84KqcqGYk6TeAHKL" alt="DIY Phone Farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 15.How to install APK to mobile phones in batches?

Press Ctrl+A to select all phones, then double-click a window and click Install APK. A window will pop up to select the APK you want to install, then click Install. Note:

\* The speed of installing APK is related to the size of the APK file.

<figure><img src="/files/WTGvfD9aXJsCeG0EXGXF" alt="How to build phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/A5c98BJcMtK9ujxzTLtR" alt="How to build phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 16.How to distribute text in batches?

Select the device to which you want to distribute the text.

<figure><img src="/files/sCX8dYcwVK1j8aliTlNr" alt="How to build phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Use separator "----" to customize fields such as "account email address, phone number, etc." Each line corresponds to one device.After setting, click the icon to enter.

<figure><img src="/files/pUFLrW6gSnTcxlawNXvc" alt="phone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 17.How to use shortcut phrases?

Double-tap the screen, select the shortcut phrase, and then select the phone to enter. Tap Enter.

<figure><img src="/files/nJ8fEPrv9LVH34vUzlyT" alt="phone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 18.How to achieve automatic screen sliding?

Select the phone that needs to slide automatically, right click, select Auto, then select the direction, number of cycles, and number of seconds between each slide. Click Start.

<figure><img src="/files/xbgeKf8FO7x4GfnhtUKB" alt="phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 19.How to Use ADB Command Console

Select a single phone, or select some phones, right-click, click ADB. Then select the command

<figure><img src="/files/omB2hFF5fnJlvnPy6Yeg" alt="phone farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## &#x20;20.How to use delayed synchronization?

1. Click on the panel's more functions, then click on delayed synchronization
2. You can set the minimum time to 1 second and the maximum time to 3600 seconds, and select the operation option that needs to be delayed
3. After setting, close the pop-up window, and the operation of the mobile phone will be delayed according to the sequence number.

<figure><img src="/files/ejKhLI2JhFSnZE9Y8Ucl" alt="phone farm Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 21.How to modify phone parameters?

1. Click on the panel more functions, then click on the phone parameter option
2. The resolution option allows you to enter the phone resolution value, the DPI option allows you to enter the DPI value, and the battery option allows you to enter a value from 1-00
3. After entering the relevant values, click Confirm to modify the phone and it will take effect immediately, but the resolution and DPI displayed in the "Device List" need to restart the client to refresh.
4. The Restore Phone Default button can restore the selected device resolution and DPI to the default value of the phone, ignoring the battery

<figure><img src="/files/u4YWPvgcmywF9f8wXLy0" alt="phone farm Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 22.How to extract APK?

1. Double-click a single window. In the application list on the right control bar, place the mouse on the APP application icon that needs to extract the APK and a pop-up window will pop up. Find the Extract APK button on the pop-up window, click and confirm
2. The APK is being extracted. The larger the APK, the slower the extraction. Please wait patiently
3. After the extraction is successful, click the Open Directory button to see the extracted APK file

<figure><img src="/files/FkjJSgJD1poDhXML6iyR" alt="phone farm box some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 23.How to turn on or off small screen control via shortcut keys?

1. Use the panel switch to turn on or off the small screen control
2. Click the shortcut bar icon and click the shortcut bar description to find the small screen control shortcut key option. When turned on or off by default, press ctrl+s. You can customize the shortcut key

<figure><img src="/files/vxctXNuubggMW6sV4OE5" alt="phone farm box some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 24.Merge adb authorization files

{% embed url="<https://youtu.be/nkx3vXB9vKk>" %}

1.In the "More" pop-up window, find "Merge Authorization Options" and click

<figure><img src="/files/540t3utQwOPGLlsY2YmV" alt="phone farm box Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

2.In the first step, select the new authorization files "adbkey" and "adbkey.pub", click OK, and <mark style="color:red;">\*\*do not select the two authorization files under "C:\\Users\\Administrator\\\\.android".\*\*</mark>

<figure><img src="/files/WGBlp8JJdwTn7yX62loU" alt="phone farm box Accessories some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

3.Then connect the unauthorized chassis to the computer. Panda will help you merge the authorization files. After the merger is completed, it will automatically go to the third step.

4.The fourth step requires you to manually click the authorization box of the mobile phone window in Panda Screen Mirror, click "Allow" in all mobile phone windows, and then click the "<mark style="color:blue;">I have authorized all</mark>" button. program will complete the final merge, just click Finish.

<figure><img src="/files/dSpAJXwPby5woGOtgh42" alt="phone farm chassis some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/PNCMj0RLP3rAMzUsb3h3" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="success" %}
\*\*We will back up the previous authorization files for you to the backup folder under the Panda installation directory.\*\*
{% endhint %}

## 25.How to chang mobile phone number or mobile phone name

Right click the window you want to change, then click the number or name to modify

<figure><img src="/files/pPHiRKiUSvAhk3TSAPHf" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 26.How do I use the 【Mobile Sharing】 function?

\*\*1.Function Introduction\*\*
\\
When you need to temporarily transfer the use of your locally connected Android device to someone else, you can initiate device sharing through this feature. The system will establish a connection by generating a unique "sharing code," and the recipient can take full control of the device after entering the code. To ensure device security and business compliance, the sharing party has the highest management privileges and can check the status or forcibly revoke control of the device at any time through "Manage Sharing."
\\
\*\*⚠️Important Note:\*\*
\\
Login restrictions:Both the sharer and the receiverYou must log in to your accountThis function can only be used when logged in; the relevant entry will be unavailable if you are not logged in.
\\
Mutual exclusion logic:Once the device is in "sharing" mode, the sharing party should avoid operating the phone locally to prevent command conflicts with the recipient. Control will only automatically revert to the sharing party when the sharing party actively stops sharing or the sharing expires.
\\
\*\*2.Which version supports device sharing?\*\*
\\
You can initiate sharing, acquire devices, and manage services independently via the PC client. Supported client versions:
\\
Panda Android Screen Mirroring v9.12.213 and above
\\
\*\*3.Step 1: Initiate device sharing (sharing party)\*\*
\\
Whether you wish to lend out a single device or multiple devices, you can find the sharing option through the following methods:
\\
\*\*3.1 Access via right-click menu\*\*
\\
Applicable scenarios:When one or more connected local mobile phones are selected on the main interface.
\\
Operating instructions:mouseRight-clickSelect the device card (or right-click after selecting multiple devices), and choose from the context menu that appears."Shared devices".

<figure><img src="/files/7SfrITJIx78iFnCZZtXO" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*3.2 Setting the expiration date and generating a sharing code\*\*
\\
Operating instructions:Clicking will bring up a time selection drawer; please select "Share".Deadline/Time.
\\
Tag Management:After confirming the time, a 9-digit sharing code will be generated in a pop-up window. You can directly copy the sharing code; the system will automatically generate a default tag, or you can edit the tag for better recognition. Click "OK" to close the pop-up window.

<figure><img src="/files/frp7Nrf0PeMTsmRXwbt0" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*3.3 Changes in Machine Status\*\*
\\
Results feedback:After successful sharing, the device card interface will change: a green indicator will appear in the upper left corner.Share iconThe middle area is displayedShare codeandExpiry dateThe bottom status bar displays "Screen casting is normal".

<figure><img src="/files/auj9ODB5zbWuJ4LSbDpF" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*4.Step 2: Obtaining and using the equipment (the acquiring party)\*\*
\\
4.1 Accessing the Entry Point
\\
Operating instructions:After logging into the client, the recipient clicks on the top menu bar.More features-> Select"Get Share".

<figure><img src="/files/hfqDTyyei8NQoNqPyWqm" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*4.2 Enter the sharing code\*\*
\\
Operating instructions:In the pop-up "Get Sharing Device" window, enter the 9-digit sharing code provided by the sharing provider, and click on the right.“Identification”Button.

<figure><img src="/files/Sj4bLSnWUSFCEVza8qPW" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*4.3 Confirm Acquisition\*\*
\\
Operating instructions:After successful system recognition, a device information preview will be loaded. Click"Confirm Acquisition"Click the button, and the system will display "Retrieval successful".
\\
\*\*4.4 Getting Started\*\*
\\
Results feedback:After a short wait, the sharing device will appear in the device list. The card will display the word "Share" and the specific expiration time. Double-click to connect and control it.

<figure><img src="/files/m1qhei4hwRjHyUU4rrMb" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*5.Step 3: Managing and Stopping Sharing (by the Sharing Party)\*\*
\\
\*\*5.1 Check sharing status\*\*
\\
Operating instructions:The sharer clicks the top menu bar.More features-> Select"Management Sharing".
\\
Interface description:On the management page, you can quickly filter devices and view the current sharing status using the three tabs: "All," "Sharing," and "Expired."

<figure><img src="/files/LhPTKSsgs0YFCIiv4cta" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*5.2 Stop sharing (revoke permissions)\*\*
\\
Applicable scenarios:When it is necessary to terminate the equipment loan early, or when an anomaly is discovered and control needs to be regained immediately.
\\
Operating instructions:
\\
In the "Manage Shares" list or device list, find the device that is being shared.
\\
Click on the device cardStop sharingButton (or right-click menu to select Stop).

In the pop-up confirmation box, select the stop range (single unit or batch), and click.Confirm to stop sharing.

<figure><img src="/files/eOp40nx3S64n2i9f2ABi" alt="Panda Free Android Screen Mirroring Display &#x26; control Android on PC | some 3C "><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

# iMouse XP New version

## 1. System requirements

\* System: Windows 10 or later
\* RAM: 8GB or higher
\* CPU: Dual-core or higher, with hardware acceleration preferred
\* Display resolution: 1920×1080 or higher recommended

## 2.Installation preparation

{% hint style="danger" %}
Before downloading the installer, it is recommended to add the download directory to the Windows security exclusion list in advance
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/PcsM8gjeJMnYudz8gtiZ" alt="imouse iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

### 1.Download iMouseXP and the VC runtime library.&#x20;

<figure><img src="/files/ktMo6GExXjY4H8sBdEQS" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

### 2.Run the "VC运行库.exe" installer to complete the installation.

<figure><img src="/files/z2HKHT13DVDo7hb0ZLNK" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

### 3.Then run iMouseXP version installer, select the corresponding language, click OK.

1. Select English               2. Select the international version

<figure><img src="/files/9Cs379m1vlq31DWAikNc" alt="imouse iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

<figure><img src="/files/ZaTs5T9yZkMfjjMYDLBf" alt="imouse iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="warning" %}
During the installation process, if any pop-up window appears asking you to "Allow", "Confirm", or "Accept", please allow
{% endhint %}

## 3.Language switching

1.Click "系统设置" and change the language in the "语言" option.

<figure><img src="/files/nu7tvaFh3cl5TLfHhzp3" alt="imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 4.Deactivating and starting kernels

iMouse is divided into two parts: \\\[Core\] and \\\[Console\]\\
\\\[Console\] is an interface that provides users with computer and mobile phone interaction. After exiting the console, the kernel still keeps running.\\
\\\[Kernel\] is the core part of the iMouse that communicates with the hardware and the phone. It is a Windows service running without an interface.

{% hint style="success" %}
If the console is abnormal, you can usually try restarting the kernel.\\
If you need to change the version or uninstall the software, please disable and uninstall the kernel first.
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/NYfCkJup17wmDx2etnY4" alt="imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Or you can find the corresponding file in the installation folder directory

<figure><img src="/files/JIOHjD7hpjF0rGxeJkgo" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 5.Start and log in to the iMouse console

1. Double-click the iMouseXP icon on the desktop
2. Register an account&#x20;

<figure><img src="/files/QDkSCw8qAfzDkqiCsT48" alt="imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 6.Interface Overview

<figure><img src="/files/cTrkDxlkC0FyzgJYJkEw" alt="imousexp iphone box farm some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 7.Setup Guide

# API Documentation | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation.md)

1.API overview (Phonefarm.some3c.com)[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-1.api-overview-phonefarm.some3c.com)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This interface can use `http` and `websocket` to access and interact.The `websocket` port number is `9911`The `http` access port number is `9912`

### 1.http request method[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-1.http-request-method)

*   interface address
    

`Server address:9912/apiExample: http://127.0.0.1:9912/api`

*   Request method
    

`post`

*   Request content format
    

`json`

### 2.websocket request method[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.websocket-request-method)

Using the websocket method to request the interface can get a faster response, but it is more complicated than the http request method. All request results are within the callback function.Because it is an asynchronous method, if you want to wait for the request result before proceeding to the next step, you need to convert from asynchronous to synchronous.Each interface will have a msgid field, and the server will return it unchanged after receiving it, which is used for asynchronous to synchronous conversion.

*   interface address
    

`ws://server address:9911/clinetExample: ws://127.0.0.1:9911/clinet`

*   Request content format
    

`json` All interface calls will return success or failure. A `status` of 0 represents success, and a status of non-0 represents failure.The failure information will be explained in the `message`. The following is a list of error codes.

error codes

2.Functional interface (Phonefarm.some3c.com)[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.functional-interface-phonefarm.some3c.com)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

* * *

### 2.1 Set up device[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.1-set-up-device)

This interface is used to set the basic information of the device, such as (`usb mouse and key device`), (`group`), (`custom name`). Multiple items can be set together or only one item can be set individually.The pid and vid of (`usb mouse and keyboard device`) can be obtained by getting the callback call of the [`connected USB device list`](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.6-get-connected-usb-list)
 and [`the usb device changes`](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.5-usb-device-changed)

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Example 1 Set device custom name**
    

*   **Example 2 Set device grouping**
    

*   **Example 3 pid and vid of usb mouse and key device**
    

*   **Example 4 Set device mouse parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

After the call is successful, all properties of this device will be returned.

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.2 Remove device[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.2-remove-device)

*   **Request parameters**
    

Delete an existing device. Online devices are not allowed to be deleted.

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**

### 2.9 Capture device screen in a loop[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.9-capture-device-screen-in-a-loop)

This function can only be called using (`websocket`). After calling, the image binary data needs to be processed in the connection (callback)If you need to stop, please send this request again and set the (`stop`) value to (`true`)If you need to modify the frequency of screenshots, please send the request again and adjust the time of (`time`)After the call is successful, the kernel will actively push screenshots of the device according to the set frequency. You need to get the sent data in the callback of (`websocket`).See the specific processing ([`loop interception of the device screen and return`](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.8-loop-to-capture-device-screen-and-return)
)

*   **Request parameters**

*   **Return successfully**

### 2.24 Enable mouse parameter collection[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.24-enable-mouse-parameter-collection)

Only after calling this interface can the mobile browser access the collection page normally.

*   Collection page address `http://{kernel service ip address}:9911/api?fun=collection`
    

For example: `http://192.168.0.233:9911/api?fun=collection`

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.25 Turn off mouse parameter collection[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.25-turn-off-mouse-parameter-collection)

After calling this interface, the mobile browser cannot be opened (`mouse collection page`)This interface must be called to close every time mouse parameters are collected.

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.26 Mouse parameter collection[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.26-mouse-parameter-collection)

Before calling this interface, you must first call [`(mouse_collection_open`](https://a0bygn2bppg.feishu.cn/docx/YMFzd9BeXoSJWgxeWVacQxjmn6e#F2dOdUBB4oxRU6xYGKgcQ7MJnef)
) to enable the mouse collection interface before the mobile phone can request the collection page.Each device requires a mouse parameter for precise positioning. By default, it will automatically match from the general library. If it cannot match, you need to manually collect the mouse parameters.Because this interface is time-consuming to call, it only returns a feedback that can be collected. The specific collection results are returned in the ([mouse\_collection\_cfg\_ret](https://a0bygn2bppg.feishu.cn/docx/YMFzd9BeXoSJWgxeWVacQxjmn6e#YvlVdKoUkoxC2QxcSzucZOn5n5R)
) callback.This interface does not support http calls.

*   Before collecting mouse parameters, the device needs to be correctly connected to the USB device, and the collection page must be opened in full-screen mode (running from the desktop) on the mobile phone.
    
*   Mouse parameter collection page address `http://{IP address of the kernel service}:9911/api?fun=collection`
    

For example: `http://192.168.0.233:9911/api?fun=collection`

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**

### 2.29 Send Chinese characters[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.29-send-chinese-characters)

To use this interface, you must bind the hotkey of the shortcut command. This interface can only be called asynchronously. The result returned synchronously only represents the success of the call. In fact, only the message that the callback returns the send\_text\_ok function number represents the success of the execution. Successful execution only means that the shortcut command successfully obtains Chinese characters. Characters will not be entered until 3 seconds after receiving the callback of send\_text\_ok.

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**

This interface only supports professional version calls. You must add the iMouse shortcut command to the correct phone and bind it correctly.

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.43 Set screen mirroring transmission mode[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.43-set-screen-mirroring-transmission-mode)

\[Energy Saving Mode\] saves the most traffic and CPU resources, and the image is a bit laggy (a single horizontal screen game has about 5M traffic, which is suitable for 100 mobile phone users)\[Normal Mode\] The traffic and CPU usage are not large, and the image is slightly delayed (about 10M traffic for a single horizontal screen game is suitable for users with about 50 mobile phones)\[High-performance mode\] The traffic and CPU usage are large, and the image is basically no delay (a single horizontal screen game has about 20M traffic)

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.44 Get screencast transmission mode[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.44-get-screencast-transmission-mode)

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.45 Get the coordinate point of restarting the phone[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.45-get-the-coordinate-point-of-restarting-the-phone)

Before using this interface, you must keep your phone online, install the OCR text recognition plug-in, and the iMouse hardware connection is normal.

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**
    

### 2.46 restart cellphone[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-2.46-restart-cellphone)

Before using this interface, you need to obtain the coordinate point for restarting the phone. For mobile phones of the same model, you only need to obtain it once.

*   **Request parameters**
    

*   **Return successfully**
    

*   **Return on failure**

)[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.-ws-callback-phonefarm.some3c.com)

### 3.1 Get device list[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.1-get-device-list)

All functional callbacks are described in the functional interface. The callbacks described here are actively notified by the server.

*   **Callback content format**
    

parameter name

Example value

Parameter Type

Parameter Description

fun

dev\_connect

String

function number

data

\-

Object

Return data

### 3.2 Device connection[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.2-device-connection)

This callback will be generated when the mobile phone is connected to the airplay screen mirroring service.

*   **Callback content**
    

Parameter name

Parameter description

Parameter name

Parameter description

ip

ip address of the device

width

The real width of the mobile phone screen

airhandle

The handle of the current device connected to airplay, used internally

height

The actual height of the mobile phone screen

mac

device mac address

device\_name

mobile phone model

username

The name of the local definition on the device

location

usb device mouse parameters

version

mobile phone system version

state

state > 0 means online. Because this callback is online, the state is definitely not 0.

model

device internal model

rotate

screen orientation, 0 vertical screen 1 horizontal screen

deviceid

device id

vid

usb device vid

srvname

The name of the currently connected screen mirroring service

pid

usb device pid

gid

group id

name

device name (user-defined name

### 3.5 usb device changed[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.5-usb-device-changed)

This callback is generated when the mouse-controlled USB device connected to the server changes.

*   **Callback content**
    

Parameter name

Parameter description

state

1 has a USB device connected, 0 has a USB device disconnected

vpid

The vid and pid of the disconnected or connected usb device appear

### 3.7 Callback when the mobile phone opens the collection page[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.7-callback-when-the-mobile-phone-opens-the-collection-page)

*   **Callback content**
    

Parameter name

Parameter description

deviceid

Device ID

state

Status, 0 the mobile phone has entered the collection page from the browser, 1 the mobile phone has entered the collection page from the desktop icon

### 3.8 Loop to capture device screen and return[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-3.8-loop-to-capture-device-screen-and-return)

This callback is generated by the 9912 port connection. The callback data is binary. The first byte of the returned data 261 is the device id, and the remaining bytes are image data.

*   **python decoding device id and image data**

*   Using environment python3.9
    

ios\_at\_api.py encapsulated api interface

main.py example using pyqt

main\_form.py form unit is automatically generated by pyuic through

main\_form.uimain\_form.ui qt interface file generated by QtDesigner ios\_at\_api.py needs to install the following dependent libraries

`pip install requests -i https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple/`

`pip install websocket-client -i https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple/` Examples using pyqt require the following dependent libraries to be installed

`pip install pyqt6 -i https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple/`

`pip install pyqt6-tools -i https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple/`

`pip install PyQt6-WebEngine -i https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple/`

5.Add iMouse shortcut command (Phonefarm.some3c.com)[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/api-documentation#id-5.add-imouse-shortcut-command-phonefarm.some3c.com)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Set shortcut commands
    

Settings->Shortcuts->Advanced->Allow scripts, allow sharing of large amounts of data, allow direct deletion without confirmation, allow deletion of large amounts of data![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fa0bygn2bppg.feishu.cn%2Fspace%2Fapi%2Fbox%2Fstream%2Fdownload%2Fasynccode%2F%3Fcode%3DNGFkMzFmN2E1NWFiNWJiOWUxMTgxYjBhOTdmYzk2MzJfNEZuTTd4NGxUTHZkYm5mMGhpdFR0eERVQ250TW05QlhfVG9rZW46VlVnU2JpRUNjbzY4ZGl4THZYMWN1clM0blZmXzE3MzAwNTQ0NzU6MTczMDA1ODA3NV9WNA&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ac4da904bcd99b95265c63a39c108220&sv=3)

1.  Download iMouse shortcuts
    

Open the iMouse console control window->Open the QR code->right-click to copy->send to mobile browser->open->get the shortcut command->modify the IP address in the shortcut command->run the shortcut command once->allow all

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fa0bygn2bppg.feishu.cn%2Fspace%2Fapi%2Fbox%2Fstream%2Fdownload%2Fasynccode%2F%3Fcode%3DMmQxMTExNDY2OTIzODNhNmE1Y2I1Mjc2NDBlNWIzYjJfM3FyOVA0OU9BQkNLYkw1UmFsdG5NUTZMaHVWUURaNW9fVG9rZW46RTVLeGJOTmNmb1F0Ukd4V2xmdWNRNUVYbkpoXzE3MzAwNTQ0NzU6MTczMDA1ODA3NV9WNA&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=bebf8598f48899daa975a2cdaf6b2bf7&sv=3)

1.  Bind iMouse shortcut commands
    

Open the iMouse console control window -> Enter accessibility on the phone -> Assistive Touch -> Device -> iMouse4.x -> Customize more buttons -> In the iMouse console control window -> Click Send Binding Key

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fa0bygn2bppg.feishu.cn%2Fspace%2Fapi%2Fbox%2Fstream%2Fdownload%2Fasynccode%2F%3Fcode%3DZmI4ZmY3YWM3ODNhNDllNmQ4MDExNjA2MTM4ZDAyNDRfdlJOeDVrRmhuWm5lY0ZmMFFZWm5NQ3lWdlFNeXFnY21fVG9rZW46RUp0dmJzdTV3bzJMRVp4bFhqaGNieXl5bmJiXzE3MzAwNTQ0NzU6MTczMDA1ODA3NV9WNA&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7395544f08aa6b032641a5fc4307a133&sv=3)

Copy

    {
       "fun": "set_dev", //Function name
       "msgid": 0, //Message id, the server will return it as it is after receiving it. The websocket connection can be used to convert from asynchronous to synchronous. Directly fill in 0 in http mode
       "data": {
         "deviceid": "F4:0F:24:D8:42:24", //device id
         "vid": "520A", //usb device vid
         "pid": "000A", //usb device pid
         "gid": "140735290", //Group id
         //The crc value of the mouse parameter of the USB device. Do not fill in this parameter randomly. It must be collected using the mouse parameters or matched by the official library.
         "location_crc": "8BEDAD44",
         "name": "Unit No. 1" //Device name (user-defined name)
       } //Parameter content
    }

         "name": "Unit No. 1" //Device name (user-defined name)

       "message": "success" //Call returns message

         "length": 0.9, //Sliding distance percentage, the default 0.9 will slide from 10% of the screen to 90%, if filled in 0.8, it will slide from 20% of the screen to 80%, automatically calculate the starting and ending coordinates x, y values, and randomly transform the starting X, Y and ending X, Y values
         "sx": 102, //Start coordinate
         "sy": 8, //Start coordinate Y (if you do not fill in this value when sliding up or down, the sliding percentage will be used to automatically calculate it internally)
         "ex": 117, //End coordinate X
         "ey": 401, //End coordinate Y
         "for": 0 //How many times to slide to the specified position in the loop (the sliding distance/number of loops will be automatically used internally to slide to the specified position multiple times. The number of left and right sliding loops is 4, and up and down is 6)
       }, //Parameter content
       "fun": "swipe", //Function name

           "first_color": "",//The color format is "RRGGBB-DRDGDB|RRGGBB-DRDGDB|…………", such as "123456-000000"\
           "offset_color": "",//Offset color can support any number of points\
           "similarity":0.8, //similarity\
           "dir":0 //Search direction 0: left to right, top to bottom 1: left to right, bottom to top 2: right to left, top to bottom 3: right to left, from bottom to top\
         }]
       }
    }

    def _binaryEvent(self, data: bytes): #Websocket binary data reception
             deviceid = data[:261].decode(encoding="utf-8").split('\x00')[0] #Get device id
             isjpg = int.from_bytes(data[261:265], "little") #Is it in jpg format?
             img_data = data[265:] #Picture data

Copy

    import base64
    import gzip
    import io
    import ios_at_api # Import ios_at_api module
    import time
    
    
    # Save base64 string as image
    def base64_to_bmp(data: dict):
         image_base64 = data['data']['img']
         is_gzip = data['data']['gzip']
         is_jpg = data['data']['isjpg']
         imgdata = base64.b64decode(image_base64)
         if is_gzip:
             with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=io.BytesIO(imgdata)) as f:
                 imgdata = f.read()
         if is_jpg:
             img_path = "{}.jpg".format(time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d-%H%M%S", time.localtime()))
         else:
             img_path = "{}.bmp".format(time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d-%H%M%S", time.localtime()))
         with open(img_path, 'wb') as f:
             f.write(imgdata)
         del data['data']['img']
         print(data)
         print("Save image successfully: " + img_path)
    
    
    # Instantiate the HttpApi class and pass in the kernel server IP address as a parameter
    api = ios_at_api.HttpApi('127.0.0.1')
    # Call the screenshot interface
    image_data = api.get_device_screenshot(deviceid='F4:0F:24:D8:42:24')
    if image_data['status'] > 0:
         print('Screen capture call failed, reason: {}'.format(image_data['message']))
    else:
         print('Screenshot call successful')
         base64_to_bmp(image_data)

Copy

    #Text message callback function
    def on_message(data: dict):
         if data["fun"] == "get_device_screenshot":
             if data['status'] > 0:
                 print('Screenshot call failed, reason: {}'.format(data['message']))
             else:
                 print('Screenshot call successful')
                 if "img" in data['data']: # Save image data to a file
                     base64_to_bmp(data)
             return # The screenshot message is too long to print, so return here
         print("Received callback message{}".format(data))
    
    
    # Binary message callback function
    def on_binary(data: bytes):
         deviceid = data[:261].decode(encoding="utf-8").split('\x00')[0]
         isjpg = int.from_bytes(data[261:265], "little")
         bmp = data[265:]
         print("Received screen data of device {}".format(deviceid))
         if isjpg == 1:
             img_path = "{}.jpg".format(time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d-%H%M%S", time.localtime()))
         else:
             img_path = "{}.bmp".format(time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d-%H%M%S", time.localtime()))
         with open(img_path, 'wb') as f:
             f.write(bmp)
         print("Save image successfully: " + img_path)
    
    
    # Instantiate the WsApi class, passing in the kernel server IP address, text message callback function, and binary message callback function as parameters
    api = ios_at_api.WsApi('127.0.0.1', on_message=on_message, on_binary=on_binary)
    # Start connecting to the kernel
    api.start()
    # Wait for successful connection
    while not api.is_onnect():
         time.sleep(1)
    
    
    # Synchronously call the screenshot interface and use base64 string to return
    ret = api.get_device_screenshot(deviceid='5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:DE')
    if ret['status'] > 0:
         print('Screen capture call failed, reason: {}'.format(ret['message']))
    else:
         print('Screenshot call successful')
         base64_to_bmp(ret)
    
    '''
    #Asynchronously call the screenshot interface and use base64 string to return
    ret = api.get_device_screenshot(deviceid='5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:DE', sync=False)
    print(ret)
    '''
    
    '''
    # Asynchronously call the screenshot interface and use binary return
    ret = api.get_device_screenshot(deviceid='5C:1D:D9:ED:D9:DE', binary=True)
    print(ret)
    '''
    
    input("Press any key to exit")
    api.stop()

# Shortcuts | Some3C User Manual

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt)

1.Get album list[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/shortcuts#id-1.get-album-list)

### Calling[](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/xp-api-documentation/shortcuts#diao-yong-shuo-ming)

*   **Request Parameters**
    

parameter

type

illustrate

Remark

fun

String

/shortcut/album/get

Request function name

id

String

Device ID

Multiple is not allowed

album\_name

String

Album Name

Leave it blank to get the photo album of the most recent project.

num

Integer

Get the number of entries

Default 10

outtime

Boolean

Timeout

The timeout for returning data, in milliseconds, the default is 15 seconds

*   **httpGet request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request GET \
      --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/shortcut/album/get?id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29'

*   **httpPost request example**
    

Copy

    curl --request POST \
      --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \
      --header "content-type: multipart/form-data" \
      --form "fun=/shortcut/album/get" \
      --form "id=6C:8D:C1:67:04:29" \
      --form "num=1"

*   **httpPost json request example \[websocket can also send the same json request\]**

# Panda Forward

## Features

Panda Forward is a P2P intranet penetration tool based on WebRTC technology, allowing you to easily achieve TCP port forwarding across local area networks. Whether you want to remotely access your home computer or connect to your home phone from the office, Panda Forward helps you establish secure and fast point-to-point connections.

<figure><img src="/files/8B7XpdxrmaNSovdocUDd" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

Click to download  (Windows  /  Mac OS)

1\. API request address[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/api-documentation#id-1.-api-request-address)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ws://127.0.0.1:22222/

2\. API request structure[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/api-documentation#id-2.-api-request-structure)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Both the request and response are JSON data structures, and the request parameters are of string type unless otherwise specified.

1.  Request example:
    

Copy

    {
        "action":"pushEvent",
        "davices":"all",
        "data":{
            "type":"2"
        }
    }

1.  Response example:
    

Copy

    {
        "code": 10000,
        "message": "SUCCESS",
        "data": null
    }

3\. API public parameters[](https://doc.some3c.com/panda-manual/api-documentation#id-3.-api-public-parameters)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

type

Is it required?

Example value

string

YES

devices

yes

Device serial number/serial number, all means all, multiple device serial numbers are separated by English commas, if there is no serial number, you can use \[IP:port\] to pass the value. You can view it in the device list-serial number, or use \[Get Device List\] to obtain it.

data

json

no

2.How to test the API interface functions? Search for "websecoket online testing tool" in your computer browser and go to https://websocketking.com/.

> 1.Panda software needs to be kept running.
> 
> 1.Enter the server address and click Connect.
> 
> 2.Enter the API statement and click send to execute.

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FTCq65lxN5jK6Vri5smXZ%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6b98b833-583e-4ec8-9e53-e64c28d8b9f1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=709fbadbf52856016577d1c8eb379fd1&sv=3)

3.What to do if the API interface is unusable?

> Cause Analysis
> 
> 1.The devices field was not entered correctly. 2.The client has enabled safe mode or accessibility mode.

1.When the device is connected via USB, the device serial number should be entered in the devices field.

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252F6lbkoGEIeuwd37KYIK9I%252Fimage.png_1759223557.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2c7c00f4-dbe2-45b8-88f5-340ba632a0bf&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7a027b949df7b9b01592f487c97ccd05&sv=3)

2.When the device is connected via WiFi or OTG mode, the device should be filled with the IP address and port number.

![](https://doc.some3c.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1443965649-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252Fnr7mODcx4OmqI75xOUOV%252Fuploads%252FHHEODjbwDWn8SIOZn7pk%252Fimage.png_1759223563.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1668caf4-8637-4ff5-92a5-d3422a127065&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3eae8386cf4c65e4e7bd5a3c6e59b276&sv=3)

3.Safe mode and accessibility mode disable USB debugging on the phone, preventing the execution of interface commands.

List all provided APIs

serial number

API

ACTION

illustrate

1

Get device list

list

Get the list of currently connected devices

2

Update device name and number

updateDevices

Modify the name and sorting number of the device

3

Execute adb command

adb

To execute the adb shell command, you only need to pass the command after \[adb shell\], there is no need to pass \[adb shell\]

4

screenshot

screen

Capture the picture of the current frame of the mobile phone and save it in the \[D:\\Pictures\] directory of the computer by default.

5

screen control

pointerEvent

Screen control operations 0 press 1 lift 2 move 4 scroll wheel up 5 scroll wheel down 6 slide up 7 slide down 8 slide left 9 slide right

Quick operation

pushEvent

Shortcut operations of mobile phone buttons 1Task management 2Home screen 3Return

7

Send to clipboard

writeClipBoard

Send text to phone clipboard

8

File upload

uploadFile

Upload files to mobile phone

9

Download files to computer

pullFile

Download the mobile phone files to the computer and save them in the \[D:\\Downloads\] directory of the computer by default.

Application list

apkList

Get mobile application list

11

Install APK

installApk

12

Uninstall APK

uninstallApk

Uninstall app

13

Start application

startApk

Launch the mobile app

14

Stop application

stopApk

Close mobile app

15

Get input method list

imeList

Get the input method currently installed on the phone

16

Install screencast input method

installInputIme

Silently install the screen projection input method (the screen projection input method will be automatically installed when the mobile phone is connected, and there is no need to call it normally)

17

Select input method

selectIme

Select current input method

18

Enter text

inputText

To enter text, the current mobile phone needs to be on the input page, and the current input method is the screen projection input method.

Create group labels

Delete group label name

Add device to group label

> Install the projection keyboard. This input method is required for text input. Generally, it will be automatically installed after connecting to a mobile phone and does not need to be called. Installation is silent, but some brands of mobile phones may pop up installation verification, which needs to be completed before installation. **1 Request parameters**

yes

yes

no

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://doc.some3c.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://doc.some3c.com/iphone-farm-setup/iphone-farm-settings.md).

# iPhone Farm Settings

{% hint style="success" %} <mark style="color:green;">\*\*Supports all models of iPhone 6s and above IOS system 13.4 and above\*\*</mark>
{% endhint %}

## For Usage Notes

{% hint style="warning" %}
\*\*Note the correct orientation of the default USB small board. If you insert the small board incorrectly and cause a short - circuit burnout, you will bear the risk yourself.\*\*
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/R7rNOk27YkMfyvPRV0YT" alt="iphone box  phone  farm  some3c.com" width="563"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="danger" %} <mark style="color:$primary;">\*\*Do not allow any cable ends or metal parts to come into contact with the yellow PCBA area, as this may cause a short circuit in the components and damage to the chassis.\*\*</mark>
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/Fk1heywbAbZDZtVXGNWU" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## 1.Connect iPhone to control panel

Connect the USB  C cable to the "电脑" port on the right side of the control board.

<figure><img src="/files/ZOi1Uy4mkTPlcfWTod2V" alt="Controlling iPhone via computer some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" icon="cart-shopping-fast" %}
\[\*\*Click to purchase the control board\*\*\](https://some3c.com/products/iphone-jailbreak-free-central-control-ios-automation-script-otg-game-studio-hardware-synchronizer-screencast-control-board)
{% endhint %}

## 2.iPhone Setting

### 1.Enable Assistivetouch

\*\*Settings-Accessibility-Touch-Assistivetouch(ON)-(AlwaysShowMenu-close Tracking Sensitivity -Max)\*\*

<figure><img src="/files/ec8y6mzrxo275nZdJwy7" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

### 2.Enable Keyboards

\*\*Settings-Accessibility-Keyboards-Full Keyboard Access(On)-Auto Hide(1s)\*\*

<figure><img src="/files/QFmYe6dwrcdwNK592kbK" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

### 3.Enable Trackpad\\&Mouse

\*\*Settings-General-Trackpad\\&Mouse-Tracking SPeed -Max\*\*

{% hint style="warning" %}
This option will only appear if the phone, hardware, and computer are connected.
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/Yzgf0urQQNo7Omh0rnum" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

### &#x20;4.Display\\&Brightness

\*\*Settings-Display\\&Brightness-Brightness(Lowest)-Auto-Lock(Never)-View(Standard)\*\*

<figure><img src="/files/aKLvVFsz8qm5sSXrbMBx" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

### 5.Lock screen orientation

\*\*Pop up the control center, open the horizontal and vertical lock, and light up the icon. And close StanBy\*\*

<figure><img src="/files/AdQH2n8Qi9lQqKtf26NI" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

### 6.Double icon

\*\*Layout of the control center is based on the hierarchical layout of the corresponding model in the figure below.\*\*

The core is that there must be "two rows of icons" at the bottom. It doesn't matter what function icons they are. Settings-->Control Center (+ and - will do), (note that this setting affects automatic screencasting)

<figure><img src="/files/8sSJJl3deYPPvgAosnu1" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

### 7.Screen Mirroring “iMouseXP-\\\*\\\*\\\*”

Then select Mirror from the Control Center.

{% hint style="warning" %} <mark style="color:orange;">The phone must be on the same network as the computer. After mirroring, the phone screen will appear on the computer.</mark>

<mark style="color:orange;">(If it does not appear, then please check the network.)</mark>
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/j7xgPLxF7u8Q5klRWLZP" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

### 8.Video demonstration

{% embed url="<https://youtu.be/nhpSHuqlKYQ>" %}
some3c.com
{% endembed %}

## 3.Computer software settings

### 1.PC and iphone must be same network

Make sure that the mobile phone and computer are on the same network segment. If the system is brand new, please install the VC runtime library.exe first

### 2.Installation environment

If the computer is equipped with other anti-virus software such as 360 or Windows Security. If so, be sure to trust (iMouse Console Professional Edition) or exit. Otherwise the software will not work properly. Recommended to uninstall

### 3.Video demonstration

{% embed url="<https://youtu.be/3CTRhCrO5d0>" %}
some3c.com
{% endembed %}

### 4.Bind hardware

#### 1.After completing screen mirroring-Open the computer software- select phone Right click-Bind hardware-Select the device ID, for example"<mark style="color:orange;">820A\\\*\\\*\\\*</mark> | <mark style="color:orange;">920B\\\*\\\*\\\*</mark> | <mark style="color:orange;">1020A\\\*\\\*\\\*</mark> |<mark style="color:orange;">1120A\\\*\\\*\\\*</mark> etc...

<figure><img src="/files/12snOPYbHHNzaa27b273" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

{% hint style="info" %} <mark style="color:orange;">\*\*If you are not sure which Device ID is bound to the mobile phone, then you can unplug the mobile phone cable and plug it in again.\*\*</mark>
{% endhint %}

<figure><img src="/files/BcsYYEj1S0pnD6moOM77" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption><p>some3c.com</p></figcaption></figure>

#### 2.There will be a prompt in the (Log window)system log about <mark style="color:orange;">Device ID</mark> associated with the iPhone.

<figure><img src="/files/XHKBxV6GrZGJNGj1xUTe" alt="imouse settings some3c.com"><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*3.Then select the phone-right-click-Bind hardware  The corresponding device ID\*\*

### 5.Mouse sensitivity detection-settings

1.Select the phone with successful screen mirroring-->right-click the mouse-->set mouse parameters

> <mark style="color:orange;">\*\*Check your iOS version to make your choice\*\*</mark>
>
> <mark style="color:orange;">\*\*Please select "1" for IOS version 14.2 or above\*\*</mark>
>

## 4.Internet settings via SIM Card

To access the internet using cellular data, please refer to the following steps.

1. Open Settings -> Wi-Fi -> Connected Wi-Fi -> Edit
2. IPv4 address -> Click Edit and select Manual Mode
3. Enter the IP address and subnet mask. Do not enter the router/gateway field! Do not enter the router/gateway field!&#x20;
4. Save and the changes will take effect immediately without affecting screen mirroring. If using a wired connection, the entry point is Settings -> Ethernet.

{% embed url="<https://youtu.be/Vyo96wZ72Gs>" %}

# XP API Documentation

This interface can be accessed and interacted using http and websocket\\
\`websocket\` and \`http\` port numbers are "\`9911\`"

## http request <a href="#http-qing-qiu-fang-shi" id="http-qing-qiu-fang-shi"></a>

\* \*\*Interface address\*\*\\
  \`iP address:9911/api\`\\
  Example:\`http://127.0.0.1:9911/api\`
\* \*\*Request method\*\*\\
  \`get\` , \`post submitjson\`,\`post form\`

## Websocket request <a href="#websocket-qing-qiu-fang-shi" id="websocket-qing-qiu-fang-shi"></a>

Using websocket to request an interface can get a faster response, but it is more complicated than http request. All request results are in the callback function.
\\
Because it is an asynchronous method, if you want to wait for the request result before proceeding to the next step, you need to perform asynchronous to synchronous conversion.
\\
Each interface will have a msgid field, which will be returned as is after the server receives it, and is used for asynchronous to synchronous conversion.5

\* \*\*Interface address\*\*\\
  \`ws://server address:9911/api\`\\
  example\`:ws://127.0.0.1:9911/api\`
\* \*\*Request content format\*\*\\
  \`json\`

All interfaces will return success or failure after calling. \`status\`200 represents success, and other values ​​represent failure. The failure information will be in\`message\` the result of the call , which represents the actual execution result.\`datadatacode\`

| code | describe                                                       |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 0    | success                                                        |
| 1    | id does not exist                                              |
| 2    | The device is not bound to hardware                            |
| 3    | Hardware not connected                                         |
| 4    | Collection failed                                              |
| 5    | A device is collecting                                         |
| 6    | This device ID is not collecting mouse                         |
| 7    | The device ID does not exist or the device is online           |
| 8    | Scan code to open successfully                                 |
| 9    | Enter the collection page                                      |
| 10   | Hardware id is empty                                           |
| 11   | No mouse parameters                                            |
| 12   | Hardware and mobile phone connection                           |
| 13   | The hardware is not connected to the phone                     |
| 14   | Start working                                                  |
| 15   | No coordinates                                                 |
| 16   | The hardware is not working (unknown error)                    |
| 17   | Retry on failure                                               |
| 18   | Failure stop                                                   |
| 19   | Hardware and computer connection                               |
| 20   | There is hardware disconnected from the computer               |
| 21   | At work                                                        |
| 22   | Equipment online                                               |
| 23   | Hardware unbound device                                        |
| 24   | Hardware not turned on                                         |
| 25   | The device is offline                                          |
| 26   | Screenshot failed                                              |
| 27   | Path does not exist                                            |
| 28   | Plugin not connected                                           |
| 29   | Calling the plugin interface times out                         |
| 30   | Call timeout                                                   |
| 31   | OCR did not recognize the text                                 |
| 32   | No text found                                                  |
| 33   | fail                                                           |
| 34   | Please remove the device in the group first                    |
| 35   | The file list is empty, or none of the files in the list exist |
| 36   | Parameter error                                                |
| 37   | Login Failed                                                   |
| 38   | Registration failed                                            |
| 39   | Password reset failed                                          |
| 40   | Failed to send verification code                               |
| 41   | Incompatible commands                                          |

# Python library

## 1.Quick <a href="#kuai-su-kai-shi" id="kuai-su-kai-shi"></a>

\* Before use, use pip to install the imouse-py package
\* \`pip install imouse-py\`

## 2.Use the API class basic interface <a href="#shi-yong-api-lei-ji-chu-jie-kou-diao-yong" id="shi-yong-api-lei-ji-chu-jie-kou-diao-yong"></a>

\`\`\`python
import imouse
from imouse.types import MouseSwipeParams

# Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the api instance. All interfaces provided by iMouse are called in the api instance.

# Perform mouse operations through the methods in the API class
api.mouse\_click("FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8", "", 100, 100)  # Left click screen coordinates (100, 100)
api.mouse\_swipe("FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8", params=MouseSwipeParams(  # Swipe up the screen from the bottom 10% to 90%
    direction='up',  # 
    len=0.9
))

\`\`\`

## 3.Easier calling through helper class (recommended)

### 1.API provided by Console

Provides access to device management and global operations:

\* \*\*Device\*\*: Device Management
\* \*\*AirPlay\*\*: Screen projection connection and configuration
\* \*\*USB\*\*: imouse Hardware Management
\* \*\*Group\*\*: Group management
\* \*\*ImConfig\*\*: iMouse Global configuration management
\* \*\*User\*\*: iMouse Account Management

\`\`\`python
import imouse

# Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the API instance

helper = imouse.helper(api)  # Get the helper class instance

console = helper.console  # Get a console instance
ret = console.device.list\_by\_id()  # Get a list of all devices
print(ret)
# Cast screen to designated device
ret = console.airplay.connect('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8,FD:9E:10:3A:FE:E0')
if ret:
    print('成功')
else:
    print(f'失败:{console.error\_msg}')

# 断开指定设备投屏
ret = console.airplay.disconnect('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8,FD:9E:10:3A:FE:E0')
if ret:
    print('成功')
else:
    print(f'失败:{console.error\_msg}')

# 投屏所有离线设备
ret = console.airplay.connect\_all()
if ret:
    print('成功')
else:
    print(f'失败:{console.error\_msg}')
\`\`\`

### 2.API provided by device

Provides control over individual devices:

\* \*\*Image\*\*: Device image operations, such as screenshots, image search, text recognition, etc.
\* \*\*KeyBoard\*\*: Keyboard operation
\* \*\*Mouse\*\*: Mouse Operation
\* \*\*Shortcut\*\*: Shortcut command operation

\`\`\`python
import imouse
from imouse.utils import file\_to\_base64
from imouse.types import MouseSwipeParams

# Connect to the iMouse server (default address is localhost)
api = imouse.api(host="localhost")  # Get the API instance

helper = imouse.helper(api)  # Get the helper class instance

device = helper.device('FA:9E:10:3A:FE:E8')  # Get the device instance by device ID

# You can also get a list of all instances through the devices method
# device\_list = helper.devices()
# device = device\_list\[0\]

# screenshot
ret = device.image.screenshot()
if ret:
    print('截图成功')
    with open('test.bmp', "wb") as f:
        f.write(ret)
else:
    print(f'截图失败:{device.error\_msg}')

# Find images through opencv
img\_str = file\_to\_base64("test1.bmp")
ret = device.image.find\_image\_cv(\[img\_str\])
if len(ret) > 0:
    print(f"找图成功\[{ret\[0\].centre\[0\]},{ret\[0\].centre\[1\]}\]")
else:
    print(f'找图失败:{device.error\_msg}')

# 向上滑动屏幕
ret = device.mouse.swipe(MouseSwipeParams(direction='up', len=0.9))  # Swipe from the bottom 10 percent of the screen to 90 percent of the screen
if ret:
    print('滑动成功')
else:
    print(f'滑动失败:{device.error\_msg}')

\`\`\`

### 3.iMouse Event Handling <a href="#imouse-shi-jian-chu-li" id="imouse-shi-jian-chu-li"></a>

\`\`\`python
from typing import List
import imouse
from imouse.api import event
from imouse.models import DeviceInfo, UsbInfo, UserData, ImServerConfigData


@event.on("im\_connect")
def im\_connect(ver: str):
    print(f"\[Event\] Connect to kernel successfully: {ver}")


@event.on("im\_disconnect")
def im\_disconnect():
    print(f"\[Event\] Disconnected from kernel")


@event.on("dev\_connect")
def dev\_connect(device\_info: DeviceInfo):
    print("\[Event\] A device is connected" + device\_info.device\_id)


@event.on("dev\_disconnect")
def dev\_disconnect(device\_info: DeviceInfo):
    print("\[Event\] A device was disconnected->" + device\_info.device\_id)


@event.on("dev\_rotate")
def dev\_rotate(device\_info: DeviceInfo):
    print("\[Event\] The device rotates->" + device\_info.device\_id)


@event.on("dev\_change")
def dev\_change(device\_info: DeviceInfo):
    print("\[Event\] Equipment changed->" + device\_info.device\_id)


@event.on("dev\_delete")
def dev\_delete(deviceid\_list: List\[str\]):
    print(f"\[Event\] Device deleted->{deviceid\_list}")


@event.on("group\_change")
def group\_change(gid: str, name: str):
    print(f"\[Event\] There is a group change->{gid},{name}")


@event.on("group\_change")
def group\_change(gid: str, name: str):
    print(f"\[Event\] There is a group change->{gid},{name}")


@event.on("group\_delete")
def group\_delete(gid\_list: List\[str\]):
    print(f"\[Event\] Group deletion->{gid\_list}")


@event.on("usb\_change")
def usb\_change(usb\_info: UsbInfo):
    print(f"\[Event\] USB device changed->{usb\_info}")


@event.on("airplay\_connect\_log")
def usb\_change(message: str):
    print(f"\[Event\] Automatic screen projection log->{message}")


@event.on("user\_info")
def user\_info(data: UserData):
    print(f"\[Event\] User information status->{data}")


@event.on("im\_log")
def im\_log(message: str):
    print(f"\[Event\]iMouse event->{message}")


@event.on("error\_push")
def error\_push(message: str, call\_fun: str):
    print(f"\[Event\] iMouse Error Log->{message},{call\_fun}")


@event.on("im\_config\_change")
def im\_config\_change(config: ImServerConfigData):
    print(f"\[Event\] iMouse kernel configuration changed->{config}")


@event.on("logout")
def logout():
    print(f"\[Event\] iMouse account exit-")


@event.on("dev\_sort\_change")
def dev\_sort\_change(sort\_index: int, sort\_value: int):
    print(f"\[Event\] iMouse device list sorting changed->{sort\_index},{sort\_value}")


api = imouse.api(host='192.168.9.9')

\`\`\`

### 4.Configuring iMouse Log Output

\`\`\`python
import logging
from imouse.utils import logger

logger.configure(
    is\_debug=True,  # Whether to enable debug logging (info/debug will not be output when False)
    name='imouse',  # Log name (affects logger name and log file name)
    log\_dir='logs',  # Log directory (default logs folder)
    log\_level=logging.DEBUG,  # Log level
    log\_show\_thread\_id=False,  # Whether to display thread ID
    log\_show\_file\_and\_line=False  #Whether to display file and line numbers
)
\`\`\`

# User related

## 1.Log in

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter | type    | illustrate         | Remark                |
| --------- | ------- | ------------------ | --------------------- |
| fun       | String  | /config/user/login | Request function name |
| phone     | String  | Phone number       | -                     |
| password  | String  | password           | -                     |
| utag      | Integer | Sub-account        | 1-10                  |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/config/user/login?phone=13999999999&password=sdfsdfsdf&utag=2'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \\
  --form fun=/config/user/login \\
  --form phone=13999999999 \\
  --form password=sdfdfdsf \\
  --form utag=2 
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
    "fun": "/config/user/login",
    "data": {
        "phone":"13999999999",
        "password":"sdfsdfdsf",
        "utag":2
    }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "",
    "pass": true,
    "total\_license": 0,
    "local\_total\_license": 100,
    "dev\_num": 16,
    "dev\_online\_num": 0,
    "create\_time": 1690800970000,
    "overdue\_time": 1683288910000,
    "phone": "13999999999",
    "utag": 2,
    "info\_clock\_in\_time": 60000,
    "add\_clock\_in\_time": 60000,
    "info\_time": 885192828,
    "add\_time": 0,
    "utag\_data": "",
    "test\_num": 100,
    "user\_state": 2,
    "network\_time": 1735124455463,
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/config/user/login"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data field description\*\*

| Fields                | illustrate                                       | Remark |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------ |
| total\\\_license        | Number of authorizations                         | -      |
| local\\\_total\\\_license | Test authorization number                        | -      |
| create\\\_time          | Registration Time                                | -      |
| overdue\\\_time         | Expiration time                                  | -      |
| user\\\_state           | 1 Formal authorization, other test authorization | -      |
| pass                  | Verification passed                              | -      |
| dev\\\_num              | Number of users                                  | -      |
| dev\\\_online\\\_num      | Online                                           | -      |
| dev\\\_num              | Verification passed                              | -      |
| utag                  | Sub-account                                      | -      |

## 4.Switch sub-account

### Calling  <a href="#diao-yong-shuo-ming" id="diao-yong-shuo-ming"></a>

\* \*\*Request Parameters\*\*

| parameter | type   | illustrate          | Remark                |
| --------- | ------ | ------------------- | --------------------- |
| fun       | String | /config/user/switch | Request function name |
| id        | String | 1 to 10             |                       |

\* \*\*httpGet request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request GET \\
  --url 'http://192.168.9.9:9911/api/config/user/switch?id=1'
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost request example\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'content-type: multipart/form-data' \\
  --form fun=/config/user/switch \\
  --form id=2
\`\`\`

\* \*\*httpPost json request example \\\[websocket can also send the same json request\]\*\*

\`\`\`
curl --request POST \\
  --url http://192.168.9.9:9911/api \\
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \\
  --data '{
    "fun": "/config/user/switch",
    "data": {
      "id":"3"
    }
}'
\`\`\`

### Response  <a href="#xiang-ying-jie-guo" id="xiang-ying-jie-guo"></a>

\`\`\`
{
  "data": {
    "code": 0,
    "id": "2",
    "message": "成功"
  },
  "status": 200,
  "message": "成功",
  "msgid": 0,
  "fun": "/config/user/switch"
}
\`\`\`

\* \*\*Response data field description\*\*

| Fields | illustrate    | Remark |
| ------ | ------------- | ------ |
| id     | Subaccount id | -      |
